# Facts - standing answers

Grep this before researching anything. One line per answer: the question, what is true now,
the symbol that does it, and the doc section with the detail. The code wins: if a line here
disagrees with the source, fix the line. When you learn something this file lacks, or the user
decides something, add the line in the same turn. Symbols, never line numbers.

## Decisions (the user's)
- **The LunaLib menu shows players knobs with a tangible effect, nothing else (the user, 2026-10-27: "the luna settings menu ingame is so laggy its unusable ... players only care about knobs that have tangible impacts which arent claude jargon or internal balancing levers").** `data/config/LunaSettings.csv` went from 439 knob rows to 63 under seven headers (The War, The Hive, Fog of War, Ground War, The Factions, Your War Board, Debug and Testing), names and tooltips in plain words; every cut knob still exists in `settings.json` and `ThreatIncConfig` and is read from there - a value a player had set in the LunaLib store for a cut knob no longer applies (the default returns). Defaults unchanged, except `frontlineLinkLY` in settings.json 10 -> 12 (the CSV default the store had been serving). Retired outright (code, settings.json, store via Luna migration 22): `grandFleetsPerFaction`, `grandFleetKills`, `groundDefenseMult`, `councilMusterDays`, `councilFeintWindowDays`, `postureStagedHalfLifeDays`, `postureStagedFloor`, the rowless `outpostChance` and `homeYards`. The harness `-Knobs` only reaches knobs with a row; the rest are `settings.json`, shared by every run. Adding a row back is one CSV line (the type, default and range from settings.json).
- **Relief goes home when its system is quiet (user, 2026-10-24: once the Threat fleets are gone "they seem to stay there indefinitely").** BUILT untested. A relief with no term on station whose system has held no live Threat fleet for `threatinc_reliefQuietDays` (7) goes home (`ThreatFleetOrders.reliefQuiet`, `ThreatGroundFronts.threatInSystem`, `Order.quietSince`); a Threat fleet entering the system or a `retaskReliefs` move restarts the count; 0 keeps the old hold until the army is gone. `ground-war-orbit-control.md` "Home when the system is quiet".
- **Reinforce from the board, convoys wait off a blockade, defenders burn armaments (user, 2026-10-17 evening, "major issue with the mod": "once youve cleared space of your own colony that is under attack. there is no way to send marines to defend ... like you can when you are sieging. theres also no way to aid allied worlds under attack like reinforce with more marines. Now if the colony is blockaded the resupply convoys should not be able to enter and should circle and wait until giving up after x period ... also i dont like how marines defending human worlds dont consume armaments").** BUILT jar 22:34, untested, uncommitted. (1) A **Reinforce** button on the fronts table under any Threat army on a human world: the player's own colony by order (`ThreatConvoys.reinforceTo`, donor `reinforceDonor`, load `reinforceWants` = `ThreatGroundFronts.reliefNeed` marines + `defenderArmsNeed` armaments net of what is at sea, sized by the Supply run ladder), an ally's as aid (`ThreatAid.quoteReinforce` / `dispatchReinforce`, standing on landing); both land in the colony's stockpile, marines arming over `marineArmingDays`. (2) Every plain convoy inside the system of a colony the Threat blockades (`ThreatBlockade.penalty` > 0, half or full) holds a passive orbit at the jump-point (`ThreatConvoys.holdsOff`) until it lifts, up to `convoyBlockadeWaitDays` (30), then turns home with its cargo. (3) A colony's engaged armed marines burn `frontArmamentsPerMarinePer30Days` of heavy armaments a day from its stockpile (`burnDefenderArms`), and fight at `frontDryEffectivenessMult` with none in stock (`defendersDry`, in `colonyMarineDefense`); knob `defenderArmsEnabled`; NPC relief convoys now carry armaments beside marines (`planRelief`, `RELIEF_MIN_ARMS` 100). `ground-war-defenders.md` "Defenders burn armaments", "Convoys wait off a blockade"; `ground-war-fronts.md` "Orders from the board".
- **Why did Reinforce Max on Haven offer 0 marines (2026-10-28)?** The donor, Arkoman, had a Threat army of its own: `ThreatReserves.frontKeep` holds every marine its stalled front needs (all 1,548), and the load took its arms down to 0 regardless. Fixed: `ThreatConvoys.reinforceLoad` caps a front donor by `frontWorldSpare`, `reinforceDonor` skips a donor with no marines to send (as `ThreatAid.quoteBattle`), and the disabled reason names the colonies whose marines hold their own front.
- **The defenders choose too: Push / Dig in on a defending colony, marines counter-attack at full weight, stability out of ground battles, an overrun muster floor (user, 2026-10-17 night, after the Arkoman test: "full weight, i dont get why the swing is a problem anyway. the marines have to take districts back no or bleed the enemy dry ... the same actions should be available regardless of if you attack or defend. and if defends push they take strata/districts back. or they can hold indefinitely and grind down the enemy with dig in (they should also get entrenchment bonus but its lost if they lose a strata) ... overrun floor yes. i dont think stability should affect ground battles at all").** BUILT jar 23:46, untested, uncommitted. (1) `marineCounterAttackMult` 0.25 -> 1.0 (Luna migration v16). (2) `Theatre.COLONY.counterAttackInterval` no longer divides by stability: 40 d / military command 1.5 / force-ratio tempo, and `min(that, frontOverrunMusterDays 10)` when `counterAttackOverwhelms` (ratioPow odds > 2, strata held or not). (3) A defending colony has a stance on its front (`GroundFront.defence`): **Push** counter-attacks on the cadence, each win retaking a district; **Dig in** holds fire (no counter-attacks, `defendersHoldFire`) and earns `frontEntrenchDefenseBonus` 1.5 on the whole defence figure over `frontEntrenchDays` 30 (`defenderCoverMult`, in `COLONY.defenderStrength`), kept at `defenderEntrenchKeptFraction` 0 when the army takes a district. The player orders their own colonies from the fronts table (Push / Dig in beside Reinforce; `ThreatFactionView.BUTTON_DEFEND_PUSH` / `BUTTON_DEFEND_ENTRENCH`); NPC colonies push when a counter-attack would win and dig in when it would bounce (`tickDefenderStance`); hives have no stance. `ground-war-defenders.md` "The defenders choose too".
- **Why is a 4,091-troop swarm "stalled" against 445 marines at Haven, stance "regroup" (the user, 2026-10-27)? And what is the point of regrouping?** The 10-day checkpoint after every district (`frontCheckpointDays`): the landing stopped pushing, so the line stood and State fell through to stalled. It was the first build's orders window for the player and had become a flat pause for every side (50 days on a six-district world, a little cover regrown, no order anyone could not give anyway). DECISION (the user): dropped - the knob's default is 0, `takeStratum` picks the doctrine's stance the moment a district falls (after the strata strip), and a regrouping front in a loaded save resolves on its next tick; `STANCE_CONSOLIDATE` is only seen with the knob set. -> `ground-war-fronts.md` "Checkpoint"
- **Why are my marines pushing when I never ordered it (the user, Arkoman 183 v 1,252 and Haven 823 v 3,454, 2026-10-27)?** An unset `GroundFront.defence` read as push and `tickDefenderStance` skipped player worlds, so every player colony pushed from the day the swarm landed until the player clicked Dig in. FIXED jar 2026-10-27: on a player colony the doctrine (`doctrineDefendersPush`) picks the stance ONCE on the first tick while no order has been given (a loaded save's fronts included); the player's Push / Dig in orders then stick as before. -> `ground-war-defenders.md` "Who orders"
- **Why did Sindria's marines not push back after the hand-over (the user, 2026-10-24: "gave them and waited a week")?** The NPC defenders' stance AI still asked the old cadence's question, would one counter-attack win (`counterAttackStrength > defenseStrength`), which never says yes against a dug-in swarm, while the push-back need was sized for the marines PUSHING on the line. FIXED: `ThreatGroundFronts.doctrineDefendersPush` - NPC marines push while pushing moves the line toward them past the band, dig in otherwise unless dug in they would lose ground while pushing holds it; the player's colonies keep their orders. `defenderCoverIfDugIn` quotes the dug-in cover while they push. SECOND HALF (same day, "weeks since I gave them marines"): the ask was sized against the swarm's cover AS IT STOOD, and that cover grows for 30 days after the swarm digs in, so marines that met the ask were 85 short a month later; `reliefNeed` now sizes every marines ask (hold / push back / overrun, relief convoys, Reinforce, Request) against the swarm at FULL cover (`frontEntrenchDefenseBonus`) - a met ask stays met. -> `ground-war-defenders.md`
- **Military structures wear only under a moving push (the user, 2026-10-24).** While the landing pushes and the line moves its way, every military structure (the theatre's key structures + the shield) takes frontWearRate x the line's rate days of disruption a day; held, stalled or pushed back, the clocks recover a day a day. The old rule wore by advantage (troops / (troops + defence)) whenever the front held, and held the defence structures down while grinding. -> `suppression-balance.md`
- **Why does the swarm keep pushing while the line reads stalled (the user, Sindria 2026-10-24)?** It no longer does: `ThreatGroundFronts.doctrinePushes` is the NPC and swarm stance rule - push only while pushing moves the line forward (past `frontGrindBand`), otherwise dig in for the cover, unless dug in the line would move against the landing while pushing holds it; a pushing front that stalls breaks off (log "digs in: pushing moves nothing"), a dug-in one that could gain goes; the consolidate checkpoint reads the same rule; a dry swarm front's final push is spent whatever the line says. -> `ground-war-fronts.md`
- **Why do Volturn's marines sit dug in at 801 against the swarm's 441 (the user, 2026-10-24)?** The board compared unlike figures: the world's Force carries its dug-in cover (`defenderStrength` x `defenderCoverMult`, 534 x 1.5), the landing's did not, and the swarm dug in for weeks is 441 x `frontEntrenchDefenseBonus` 1.5 = 661 against the 534 the marines push with (`lineRateOf`: gainD = pressure - A x coverA), so pushing moves nothing and `doctrineDefendersPush` digs in. Against a fully dug-in swarm a colony pushes only past ~1.8:1 raw (pressure > 1.5A + `frontGrindBand` x (A + D)), and with both dug in the line stands for good. FIXED the display: Force and `defenderPushQuote` show the landing x `coverMult`. A "push wears cover" rule was built and REVERSED within the hour (the user: "no gradual removal of entrenchment - full wiped if the side loses a district, otherwise it increases over time while dug in regardless"): entrenchment is never worn by the enemy, diluted (the reinforcement dilution is gone) or cut by a kept fraction (`frontEntrenchKeptFraction`, `defenderEntrenchKeptFraction` retired). Then the user's two rules the same hour: **pushing spends it and past zero the push is OVER-EXTENDED** to the same degree (`overExtension` / `defenderOverExtension`, the side's figure / (1 + 0.5 x over-extension), full after `frontEntrenchDays` of pushing past zero; the board says "over-extended"), and **a district changing hands resets BOTH sides to 0**, dug in or over-extended (`takeStratum`, `loseStratum`). So two dug-in sides neither of which can move the line stand until one is reinforced, and a long push runs out of steam by itself. Both doctrines weigh their stance against the other side's best reply (`lineRate` at each pair of stances, `standing`) - the same afternoon the log showed Sindria's marines and the swarm swapping stances every few seconds, each answering the other's last move (the user: "why are they pushing"); marines pushing against a dug-in swarm with the line standing is the settled answer where dug in they would lose ground, and the tooltip says "Pushing holds the line". -> `ground-war-defenders.md`
- **The colony condition tooltip carries the marines' armaments burn (the user, 2026-10-24: "armament consumption rate ... specifically for marines"):** "Marines' armaments N d = stock / (armed x each per 30 days = X/d)", or "armaments 0: defence x0.5 dry" (`ThreatGroundWarCondition`); the board's fronts tooltip has the same line as "<World> arms".
- **Forward bases off means none, and OFF IS THE DEFAULT (the user, 2026-10-24: "sindria just created a new forward base in my save, thought we removed them completely", then "Off by default for the mod too").** The 14:14 one-shot only dismantled the standing ones; founding went on because `threatinc_frontlinesEnabled` was true in the user's LunaLib store. Now with the knob off `ThreatFrontlines.tick` also dismantles every standing link (`dismantle`, "forward bases are off": stock and garrison home), and the default is false in settings.json and the Luna row; Luna migration 17 (`LunaConfigBridge.migrateStoredDefaults`) flips a store still at true on the next launch. A player who wants them turns "Frontline Outposts Enabled" on in LunaLib.
- **No `%` in `data/config/LunaSettings.csv` text.** LunaLib passes names and tooltips through `String.format`, so "0% Accessibility" crashed the game on opening the mod options (2026-10-25, `MissingFormatArgumentException: % A` in `LunaSettingsUISettingsPanel.recreatePanel`). Write "percent".
- **Deployed, reserves, staging for X - never "stock" (the user, Sindria 2026-10-24: a levy of 2,326 read "in stock" while 1,050 fought: "don't call them stock when they aren't deployed. Call them reserves. And any being used for a siege are staging for x"; the arming ramp STAYS - "I didn't say get rid of the arming ramp").** The board's world line reads `N marines deployed`, then `World: R marines in reserve, arming; S marines staging for <system>` (`ThreatReserves.stagingMarines` = the reserve the base holds for `ThreatConvoys.stagingHive`'s siege, `stagingTarget`); the colony condition says the same. A colony under a Threat army keeps the marines its ground victory wants (`ThreatReserves.frontKeep` = `ThreatGroundFronts.deployWant`: the whole figure `reliefNeed` is the shortfall of - the line moving the defenders' way at `frontPushBackRate`, or holding where that is out of reach, then every marine) and may sortie or stage the rest (`available`, `spendable`, `ThreatSoftening.donorSpendable`, `ThreatConvoys.frontWorldSpare` now spare marines above the want; until today every marine on a front world was held by `committed`). The Sindria levy was the Diktat's invasion levy raised AT Sindria; it sailed for Mazalot only after the front collapsed (log: "Front collapsed at Sindria" then "Convoy dispatched: ... Sindria -> Mazalot (2767 marines"). -> `ground-war-defenders.md` "Marines are called up"
- **Why did Sindria's ground defence drop to vanilla's 190 the moment the siege ended (the user, 2026-10-24, screenshot: 400 base x 0.48 stability, every structure disrupted 112 d)?** The siege-state carrier (`ThreatSiegeMalus`, which restores a disrupted structure's bonus and stability in proportion) was installed only while a front stood or the world was flagged besieged; with the front gone it was removed and vanilla stripped the disrupted structures whole. Now `syncSiegeState` / `sweepSieges` keep it while ANY defence structure is disrupted, siege or no siege. -> `ground-war-verify.md` 3
- **Why did Sindria run out of armaments with Volturn next door on 1,800 (the user, 2026-10-24)?** Relief convoys did sail (Cruor sent 133 then 100, its whole spare) but the Diktat's one armaments maker is Volturn, itself under a Threat army, and a front world spared nothing (`ThreatConvoys.spare` / `reliefSpare`: hasFront -> 0). Now a front world spares heavy armaments above its own defenders' 60-day want (`frontWorldSpare` over `ThreatGroundFronts.defenderArmsWant`), both for relief convoys and ordinary logistics; marines, fuel and supplies still stay home. -> `ground-war-defenders.md` "Defenders burn armaments"
- **No siege, front or tactical bombardment disrupts the port (the user, 2026-10-24: "megaport is disrupted by sieging ... it should not be, lets simplify here. tac bombard should not effect it. only accessibility penalties come from blockades").** The port is out of both theatres' `keyStructures` (what a landing wears while it advances; it was never in the orbital slice's `fortifications`) and out of the player's danger-close barrage (`ThreatincMarketCMD`). Saturation bombardment keeps vanilla's rule (every building). A hive's port therefore stays up under siege: `portArms` rearming is gated by the blockade's import cut alone now.
- **Pushers keep their cover (the user, Volturn 2026-10-24: "really? they need 800 marines to push? i dont think so" -> "Pushers keep their cover").** `ThreatGroundFronts.lineRateOf`: `gainA = A x coverA - D x coverD`, `gainD = D' x coverD - A x coverA` - both sides fight at their entrenchment's cover whatever their stance (before, a pushing side fought at cover 1 against the other's full cover: Volturn's 379 garrison + 208 marines vs 524 troops dug in read +485 marines to push, +820 to push back, +1,824 to overrun; now about +100 / +260 / +620, and the swarm pushes Volturn with ~584 troops instead of 908). Every reader follows: the board's "Needs N to push" (`troopsFor`), the dock's tiers and relief asks (`deployWant` / `reliefNeed`), `defenderPushQuote` (no "No cover" any more). -> `ground-war-fronts.md` "The rate"
- **The control line names the holding faction, and names are coloured (the user, Volturn 2026-10-24: "volturn doesnt hold 50% sindria does. use faction name there"; "color code faction names in the tooltip dialog").** The fronts tooltip's "Control of the district: Sindrian Diktat 50%, Swarm 50%" reads the world's owner (`ThreatWarState.displayName`) in its colour and the army side in the Threat's or the landing faction's (`ThreatWarBoard.factionColor`, `setHighlightColors`). The dock's need lines (`ThreatincAidCMD.colour`) colour the world and its faction in the owner's colour, "Threat" in the swarm's and the figures in the highlight, whole words only, left to right.
- **Each faction's board opens with its Strength score (the user, 2026-10-24: "an overall score on each human factions board indicating their overall strength (tooltip gives breakdown of calculation)").** `ThreatFactionView.strengthOf` -> `Strength`, in fleet points: navy = `ThreatHulls.freeFP` + `committedFP` (at sea); army = every colony's `colonyGarrison` + `colonyMarineDefense(m, 1)` / `scoreTroopsPerPoint` (20); depots = the lesser of `factionStock` supplies / `expeditionSuppliesPerPoint` (30) and fuel / (`expeditionFuelPerPointLY` (10) x `scoreSiegeRangeLY` (10)) - the FP of sieges the depots pay. The line reads "Strength N = navy + army + depots", its tooltip one line per part with the figures. Read as the board renders, never stored; the player's own tab has it too.
- **A military structure's stability bonus is vanilla's: on while it runs, off while disrupted (the user, 2026-10-24 evening: "lets not play with the stability modifiers for disrupted ground defences or high command etc. they can just be on or off depending on disrupted or not as vanilla").** `ThreatSiegeMalus.apply` no longer restores any stability (the 50% rule `structureStabilityDisruption` of the same afternoon and the condition-scaled one from Jangala 2026-09-28 both went; the knob is gone); only the defence multiplier is restored, scaled by condition.
- **No swarm over the world, no relief fleet (the user, Sindria 2026-10-24 evening: "sindria keeps sending relief fleets to its worlds under siege, despite the worlds having no threat over them. it only needs to send them when the threat are occupying space").** `ThreatFleetOrders.reliefGoal` is 0 unless `pointsNear(market, THREAT, true)` > 0 (before: one guard of `guardFleetFP` 100 went anyway), so `reliefShort`, `reliefOwed`, `planRelief` and the coalition's `jointRelief` all stand down over a quiet orbit; a relief already on station over a quiet world moves on (`retaskReliefs`). The marines' convoys (`ThreatConvoys.planRelief`) are untouched. -> `ground-war-orbit-control.md` "Relief"
- **A front fights to the last troop (the user, Sindria 2026-10-24: "Floor 0").** The war ended the day after Sindria retook its sixth district: the dug-in swarm's Force 86 was ~57 troops x 1.5 cover, the district reset its cover, and one day's attrition took it under the old 50-troop floor (`frontMinMarines`, which also sizes landings and raids). Now `ThreatGroundFronts.groundDown` = 0 troops or under the new knob `frontCollapseTroops` (default 0); `frontMinMarines` keeps its other jobs. The three collapse sites (attrition, swarm bombardment, relief bombardment) and the console barrage read it.
- **A layer changing hands is a notice (the user, 2026-10-24: "a notification when threat or human faction take a district/strata").** `ThreatGroundFronts.announceLayer` from `takeStratum` and `loseStratum`: "District Taken" / "Stratum Retaken" etc., the swarm's deed in the Threat colour under its crest, ours blue under the faction's, second line "<who> holds N of M districts". The 2026-09-27 "no progress notices" rule stands for sieges; a layer is an outcome, not progress.
- **The Force arrow points from whoever is attacking (the user, Sindria 2026-10-24: "sindria are pushing 402 to 198 yet the arrow points at sindria").** It had pointed from the landing. Now: -> our side attacking, <- theirs, <-> both, - neither ("300 - 320", the user) (`ThreatWarBoard` Force cell; header tooltip says so).
- **No district stability penalty (the user, Sindria 2026-10-24: "districts lost penalty is applied twice").** Vanilla's ground-defence tooltip showed x0.29 "Districts lost to the ground front" AND x0.25 stability, with the stability itself carrying -5 "Districts held by the invaders": `ThreatSiegeMalus.apply` no longer touches stability (it unmodifies the old flat so saves clear it); `districtStabilityPenalty` is retired (config, settings, Luna row). **The accessibility penalty went the same evening** (the user: "remove accessibility penalty from districts held. should apply to threat worlds as well"): `districtAccessPenalty` retired too, `apply` unmodifies both old flats so saves clear them; the malus sits on hives and colonies alike, so both are covered. A held district now costs the defence share and the seized industries, nothing else.
- **The fronts-table row tooltip is the math (the user, 2026-10-24: "the exact numbers/breakdown/formula for each force number, and every other number there - no fluff").** `ThreatWarBoard.addFrontTooltip`, in the shape the user sketched the same afternoon: a lay sentence ("Swarm gaining ground at 0.15 a day: next district in 40 d" / "Line stands"), then one line a side - "Swarm 388 = 373 troops x 1.15 veteran x 0.91 poor cover (6/30 d). Needs 520 troops to push." and "Sindria 257 = (14 ground defence + 242 marines x 1.00 armed) x 1.00 no cover, attacking (0/30 d). Needs 540 marines to push." - the figure is the Force column's, "cover" is the ONE word for dug in / over-extended (`ThreatGroundFronts.coverWord`: spread thin, no, poor, some, good, full cover; the factor is entrench x over-extension x cover in one), and "Needs x to hold / push / overrun" is the next tier that side's way solved on its troops or armed marines (`troopsFor`, halving on `lineRateWith`; the ledger's ask is NOT quoted here: it is sized against the swarm at full cover and would read as a second number); the header is "<Faction> front on <world>, <system>." then "Day N - Sindria holds the line." / "Day N - Swarm gaining ground at 0.15 a day: next district in 40 d." and "Control of the district: Sindria 60%, Swarm 40%; Swarm holds 2 of 5 districts." (the landing's share of the contested layer = (pushProgress + base) / 2 base); no gain or line-formula lines (the user: "its evident whats happening"); the losses lines read THE ATTRITION RULE, one shape both sides: "Swarm attrition 2.8/d = 3.73 size x (Sindria 320 / Swarm 390) x 1/0.91 cover x 0.92 veteran." (every constant named - the user: "no magic numbers"; arms read "509 stock / (242 troops x 0.25 each per 30 days = 2.0/d)") then "Pressure 1.00 = swarm 348 / Sindria 257, max 1." (`frontPressure`; the user: "pressure factor sounds good" - a mirror of the landing's rates was built and REVERTED within the hour, the user had asked for an explanation, not a change); NO rate figures anywhere in words (the user: "stop talking about lines, i dont know what 0.15 line is"): "Day N - Swarm gaining ground: next district in 40 d", "Objective: next district to the swarm in 40 d" / "none", "Victory: swarm in 160 d (3 more districts after this one)", "Military structures +1.8 d disruption a day while the swarm advance" / "recovering 1 d of disruption a day"; Objective and Victory (days left / rate), Disruption (mean of days/cap over `militaryStructures` only - the user 2026-10-24: "shouldn't factor in Megaport, only military buildings" - and the wear rule), both losses (troops x rate x factors / 30 + orbit), both arms (stock / burn), the ask. Nothing else; the mechanism prose went to the docs.
- **The force figure is the push figure (the user, Sindria 2026-10-24: "there should be no invisible multipliers").** Sindria held with 257 and pushed with 386 because `colonyCounterAttackMilitaryMult` (1.5, a leftover of the counter-attack cadence) multiplied the push at any colony with a Patrol HQ / Military Base / High Command / Lion's Guard, disrupted or not. RETIRED: `defenderPressure` is `counterAttackStrength` (garrison + armed marines x `marineCounterAttackMult` 1.0) and nothing else; the knob, its Luna row and the tooltip's "(military command x1.5)" are gone. **Cover is by days alone (the user, 2026-10-24 evening, Sindria pushing at "1.00 good cover"): `coverMult` and `defenderCoverMult` no longer drop to 1 for a pushing side - digging in earns cover a day at a time, pushing spends it a day at a time and past zero spreads the side thin; the line, the attrition rule and the Force column all read the same number, and the cover word (`coverWord`) always matches the factor.*
- **The forward-base dismantle-on-load one-shot is GONE (the user, 2026-10-24 evening: "remove the thing that clears all forward bases every time i load game").** It had fired on every load because the once-flag lived in the save and the user kept reloading a save taken before it; `ThreatFrontlines.dismantleAllOnce` / `spreadStockHome` and the `onGameLoad` call are removed (never committed), the common file `threatinc_dismantleForwardBases.data` deleted. Forward bases found and fall as the war has it.
- **The dock is locked while the swarm holds the orbit (the user, 2026-10-24: "ALL colony actions other than the basic view info").** `ThreatincAidCMD.lockOptions`, from the gate row at every non-Threat colony (the player's too): while `ThreatGroundFronts.spaceHolder` is the Threat the menu is rebuilt once and vanilla's comm directory / bar / storage / trade / refit / fleet / hostile options and the aid hand-over are greyed with "The swarm holds the orbit over X: nothing docks until it is lifted"; Leave and the dock text stay.
- **The dock showed no "Deliver aid for the war effort" at besieged Sindria (the user, 2026-10-24).** Unexplained by reading: the rows read a market memory flag `ThreatNeeds.tick` sets every half day with a 2-day expiry. Since 2026-10-24 the three PopulateOptions rows run `ThreatincAidCMD option` instead, which adds the option at any NPC colony of a mobilised faction and logs "Dock aid option at <world>: offered, N need(s)" or "none - <why>" - read that line off the log the next time the option is missing.
- **Why did Sindria fall while both boards read "stalled" (the user, 2026-10-24)?** Inside `frontGrindBand` the State read stalled but the tick still added the rate to `pushProgress`, so a 0.05 rate took a district every 120 days unseen. FIXED: `ThreatGroundFronts.lineMoves` - inside the band the line STANDS (no progress, `daysToLineChange` -1, `pushDaysEstimate` -1, `losingGround` false). Same day the fronts table lost its side-blind columns: Defenses -> **Disruption** (the besieged world's military structures over the cap, averaged, `disruptionFraction`), Line -> **Objective** (days until THIS side takes the next layer, "-" otherwise), Falls -> **Victory** (days until THIS side wins the world, `victoryDays`: last layer taken or the landing pushed off); and the Threat tab carries NO order buttons (Push / Dig in / Reinforce / Aid) - a faction is aided from its own tab. -> `ground-war-fronts.md` (column table)
- **The fronts table is per tab, read from that tab's side; no attacker / defender words anywhere (user, 2026-10-18 00:00-00:20: "if you are on the threat tab you see all the fronts from the context of the threat. if you are on sindria tab u see all fronts from sindria perspective ... just say marines and the swarm ... call the strength tab Force actually, and have either 300 -> 700 or 400 <- 500 depending on which tab you are on").** BUILT jar 00:22, untested, uncommitted. The Threat tab lists EVERY front above the hive list (it used to show only the selected system's, under its heading - `frontRowsIn` is now unused); a faction tab its own (`frontRowsFor`) above Colony Reserves. `ThreatWarBoard.addFronts` takes the viewing faction; per row `weLanded` = the viewing side landed here. Columns: World (owner's colour), System, **Force** "ours -> theirs" (we attack) / "ours <- theirs" (they attack) / "<->" both / "-" neither - the arrow follows the attack since 2026-10-24, not who landed, Defenses, State, Held (taken by our landing, or still ours on our world), Stance / Losses / Arms (ours only; Arms "-" on the Threat tab), Counter, Falls, Space; colours flip with the side (worn defences, a repelled counter-attack, a Falls estimate are the landing's good news). No crest column. The row tooltip keeps both sides in the words Marines / Swarm / the landing / the hive, as do the colony condition tooltip, the Push / Dig in buttons, prompts and notices (an hour-long "Marines / Swarm" two-sided table in between was the misreading of this ask). `ground-war-fronts.md` "Reading a front".
- **The reinforcement leash recalls beyond twice the hold range (2026-10-13 22:00, after hw186c).** At the hold range itself (1,500) the aggressive orbiters over a besieged hive rode at 1,500-1,505 units and were recalled and blinkered every day of the siege (45 of 83 recalls in hw186c); the strays the leash exists for stood 13-15,000 units out. `ThreatColonyManager.REINFORCEMENT_LEASH_MULT` = 2; retested as hw188 from hw186c's d2847 and d3803 snapshots (flap gone, far strays still recalled). `game-runs-2.md` s53.
- **hw189 (2026-10-18, the defenders build 0fcb269e from the hw182 pre-opening snapshots): the daily swarm strike takes worlds faster than the one-frame path did** - hives 115 / 124 / 118 against hw185's 127 / 118 / 85, the humans' colonies 7 / 12 / 9 against 14 / 34 / 23, Threat ground victories 40 / 42 / 40 against 35 / 12 / 20: a daily strike finishes a world and moves on through its system window where the one-frame strike sat over one world for years (hw185b Mairaath, 1,212 fabricate lines). Dig in / push and full-weight counter-attacks are in use and did not hold the worlds. `game-runs-2.md` s54.
- **The daily strike's nothing-to-land gate must read the world's share, not troopsAboard (fixed 2026-10-18 10:45, after hw189).** An unspawned swarm strike carries 0 troops aboard (its hulls break into troops at the landing), so `ThreatStrikeFGI.dailyDay`'s gate ended every daily siege on day one with the passes landing the whole strike unbombarded (hw189c: 35 of 57 landings, 5 bombarded days). Now `Math.max(worldShare(), troopsAboard)`, the one-frame path's figure. Rerun hw191-hw196. `game-runs-2.md` s54.
- **hw191 (2026-10-18, the defenders build with the gate fixed, hw182 snapshots): the nerf lands** - hives 70 (peak 79) / 132 / 91 against hw185's 127 / 118 / 85, human colonies 35 / 33 / 27 against 14 / 34 / 23, swarm landings 17 / 30 / 46 against 55 / 18 / 39: strikes bombard 3-7 days under the beachhead gate, lose ~800 FP to the guns a world, and a third are priced at forward bases. a trends human from w3,400 (68 cores destroyed under 2,050 hive slices). More rounds before any change. `game-runs-2.md` s54.
- **Do the humans bombard a hive before landing (user, 2026-10-18)?** Mostly not. `IncursionManager.siegeOrdnance` prices ordnance for the bombard days `bombardPlan` says the landing needs, and at the landing gate (`readyToLand`: troops x `frontLandingMult` 0.75 >= 0.17 x defence) a 930-marine expedition against a hive of defence ~1,500 needs none: hw191a drew ordnance 0 on the median siege (1,703 draws, p90 952 fuel), 58 of 85 landings read "landed (dry)" on day one (`ThreatPurgeFGI.dailyDay`, `paysOrdnance` with 0 left) at full fortification condition, and the hive guns cost the humans 2,954 FP in the whole game. Nothing prices a drop against standing batteries. `game-runs-2.md` s54.
- **The daily strike never destroys a forward base (found 2026-10-18 11:55, hw191a).** `ThreatStrikeFGI.endWorld` marks the world in `siegeResolved` before running the passes, and `stationAssault` returns on `!siegeResolved.add(id)`: 0 "Station assault on" lines in every daily-path game (hw189, hw191), 3 in one-frame hw185a; hw191a spent 37 of 57 strikes (159k FP) on bases and ended facing 36 (hw185a 6). The stations that did fall (5 / 15 / 8) fell to live fleets in battle. Fix: guard the assault with its own set, not the siege's. `game-runs-2.md` s54.
- **Mechanisms are read as attempts against effects, not outcomes (2026-10-18 12:45, after the user: "every time I brainstorm you find another bug - is there stuff you aren't fully checking?").** Both of the day's bugs were paths that fired every time and did nothing (the nothing-to-land gate, the outpost exit: 36 of 36 in hw191a), invisible to a hives / colonies read and to a watcher checking one round's hypothesis. `tools/test-harness/reads/effects-ledger.sh <tag> [a b c]` pairs every logged mechanism's attempt line with its effect line per game; a 0 or 100 percent ratio is a bug candidate. Run it on every finished game. hw191 ledger: blockade hold, convoys, defender stance AI (dig in 38 / 48 / 64, push 29 / 37 / 47), relief all work; 0 repelled counter-attacks in 370 is the stance AI digging in whenever a counter-attack would lose (`ThreatGroundFronts.tickDefenderStance`), not a bug; `burnDefenderArms` and `Garrison musters` have no effect line and cannot be read.
- **The Relief and Swarm-defend leashes recall at the hold range itself (found by the ledger 2026-10-18, FIXED 13:03: `ThreatFleetOrders.LEASH_MULT` 2, `withinLeash`).** `ThreatFleetOrders.leash` recalls and blinkers a fleet beyond `nearPlanet` = ORBIT_HOLD_RANGE 1,500 while its ORBIT_AGGRESSIVE rides at 1,500-1,700: hw191 a / b / c 150 / 625 / 230 "Relief over X: ... strayed N units - recalled to the orbit" at p50 1,672 units (p90 2,242), "Swarm defend" 391 at p50 1,670 - the hw186c reinforcement defect (fixed there alone, `ThreatColonyManager.REINFORCEMENT_LEASH_MULT` 2). The siege leash (`siegeLeashRange`, 6,000+) is clean. Impact low (the relief still bombards: 230 / 576 / 1,060 bombard days), but every recall clears the fleet's assignments. Fixed as the reinforcement fix did: the leash leaves a fleet alone within 2x the hold range; first read due in the round after hw193.
- **The daily strike destroys forward bases again (fixed 2026-10-18 12:11, ebdca078; CONFIRMED hw193).** `ThreatStrikeFGI.stationAssault` is guarded by its own `stationsAssaulted` set and the outpost branch of `dailyDay` sits below the orbit fight, so a base's station and guards fight the strike a day at a time (`hostileFleetsNear` counts the station fleet; `ThreatAbstractBattle` never damages it) before the assault. hw193: 12 of 12 outpost exits assaulted and destroyed their base, 3 strikes beaten in orbit by a base; at the same war day c is level with the hw185 baseline (73 vs 71 hives), b 56 vs 65, a 47 vs 89 (46 strikes at colonies, 14 of 32 beachheads overrun). `docs/frontlines.md` has the path, `game-runs-2.md` s54 the read.
- **Why the Threat never repels a counter-attack (2026-10-18, hw191 / hw193 ledgers): sizing, not a bug.** `IncursionManager.beachheadNeeded` lands enough to survive the first counter-attack at the 2:1 overrun line with `siegeBeachheadMargin` 1.25 - ~0.83 of the defence in marines, 0.63 after `frontLandingMult` 0.75 - and still does (first-blow overruns are rare). But `marineCounterAttackMult` went 0.25 -> 1.0 in 0fcb269e, so a colony counter-attacks with the whole defence (`counterAttackStrength` = garrison + marines x 1.0) and `tickDefenderStance` only attacks when it wins: 307 of 370 counter-attacks at 1.5-3x odds, a stratum lost a cadence, half the Threat fronts overrun. Options for the user in `game-runs-2.md` s54 (size to repel at 1:1 recommended; dig in on landing; the knob back to 0.5). Not built.
- **The defenders' armaments are logged (2026-10-18 13:03).** "Defenders of X: N armed marines, A armaments, burn B a day (D days)" at a Threat landing (`ThreatGroundFronts.deploy`), "Defenders of X dry: ... fight at x0.5 until armaments arrive" and "Defenders of X rearmed: ..." once a transition (`burnDefenderArms`, `GroundFront.defendersDry`). The census lists mobilised factions only: a colonies total from census lines is the mobilised factions' colonies (hw191a at w3,085 lists three factions; the hw191 end figures 35 / 33 / 27 counted the four main factions).
- **DECISIONS 2026-10-18 13:20 (the user): "size to repel", and "if a counter-attack is incoming attackers dig in and entrench".** Built as `threatinc_beachheadRepels` (the beachhead line at 1:1 x `siegeBeachheadMargin` instead of the 2:1 overrun odds - `BattleRules.beachheadOdds`, `IncursionManager.beachheadNeeded`, `ThreatGroundFronts.beachheadTroops` / `beachheadSurvives` / `troopsToLand`; ~2x the marines a landing carried, both sides) and `threatinc_frontBraceWhenBeaten` (`shouldBrace` tests `beatenIfPushing` through `counterAttackDanger`, ground held or not; the 2026-09-08 "press on and take the setback" is the knob-off rule). `docs/ground-war-fronts.md` "Bracing". Untested at build; first round hw194.
- **The brace and the NPC defenders' dig-in chased each other (found 2026-10-18 13:45 in hw194, FIXED in source, hw195 the trial).** `shouldBrace` returned false while `defendersHoldFire` (the NPC colony dug in), but `tickDefenderStance` digs in BECAUSE the front took cover and pushes again the day it climbs out, so the two flipped daily: brace -> defenders dig in -> "no blow coming" -> resume at 0 d of progress -> defenders push -> brace (hw194c Ragnar Complex 26 braces, 93 in the game; a Laicaille Habitat 21). Now only a player-owned colony's hold-fire order short-circuits the brace; against an NPC colony `counterAttackDanger` already asks what its defenders would do to a pushing front. Ledger row "front braces -> resumes" counts flapping worlds (>5 braces).
- **hw194 (2026-10-18, repel line + brace-when-beaten, the brace flapping): hives at w3,000 64 / 69 / 86 against hw193's 43 / 56 / 73 and hw185's 86 / 66 / 73; end 72 / 129 / 102.** The repel line lifts the Threat in all three games (c back level with its baseline at w3,000, above it at the end); a stays contested because the humans land to the same line and eradicated 23 hives. Repelled stays 0 by construction against NPC colonies (their AI attacks only when it wins); the line's effect shows as fewer overruns per landing and more victories. Every flapping Threat front in a took nothing (4 worlds, 56 / 38 / 36 / 27 braces), so hw195 on the fixed brace is the number to read.
- **hw195 (2026-10-18, the brace fixed): the brace works at the front, the sector is one game's noise.** Braces 36 / 7 / 23 (hw194 214 / 158 / 279), no daily flap; Threat victories per landing 56 / 57 / 73 percent (hw193 38 / 29 / 63), overruns per counter-attack 9 / 9 / 26 percent (hw194 14 / 16 / 18). Hives at w3,000 52 / 57 / 78, end 67 / 95 / 87 - b swung 129 -> 95 hives on the same snapshot, so rank builds on several rounds, not one. a (AmaruDugas) trends human in every round since hw191: the humans eradicate 12-23 hives a game there on the same repel line.
- **DECISION 2026-10-18 14:50 (the user, after hw195: "let's proceed with the marines landing loss change"): THE DROP UNDER THE GUNS.** A landing loses `threatinc_dropLossPerGunDefence` 0.3 (hive `threatinc_hiveDropLossPerGunDefence` 0.3) x the defence the batteries still add (`gunDefence`, the return fire's figure - worn guns take less), at most `threatinc_dropLossMaxFraction` 0.6 of it; both sides, once per landing or reinforcement (`ThreatGroundFronts.dropped` from `landOrReinforce` and the player's menu, never on fabricated troops). Every gate sizes for the survivors (`afterDrop` in `readyToLand` / `beachheadSurvives`, `landingFor` in `beachheadTroops` / `troopsToLand` / `IncursionManager.raidStrNeededAt`). `ground-war-sieges.md` "The drop under the guns". Ledger row "drops under the guns". Trial hw197 (set A).
- **hw197 (2026-10-18, the drop under the guns): the toll lands on the Threat, barely on the humans.** Threat drops lost 5-7 percent (2,299 / 3,420 / 2,673 of 35k / 66k / 47k marines), mostly on intact colony guns; human drops on hives lost 1-3 percent (51 / 169 / 37 in 1 / 6 / 2 of 3 / 9 / 3 landings) because most hives carry no battery growths (gun defence 19-169). Human fronts deployed fell to 3 / 9 / 3 (hw195 15 / 2 / 14 - the planner carries the toll in its ask) and hives eradicated to 2 / 2 / 1 (hw195 12 / 2 / 7), hives 95 / 88 / 119 at the end (hw195 67 / 95 / 87) - one round, inside the variance. To make the rule bite the humans, raise `threatinc_hiveDropLossPerGunDefence` (0.9 = the return fire's 3x) or give more hives guns; the user's call.
- **Do the Threat send physical convoys to feed their worlds (user, 2026-10-18)?** No, and neither do the humans for theirs. Hive worlds are fed each poll from the one shared Threat supplies stock (`ThreatColonyUpkeep.feed`), pooled transfers paying `haulPerUnit` fuel per unit per ly; a forward base pays its size upkeep (`ThreatFrontlines.feedSize` - the hive's own table and starve rule since 2026-09-30) and its garrison's upkeep (`payUpkeep`) from its own stock, then its home base, then faction markets whose stock reaches it. Real convoys: founding kits (seeding swarm / founding convoy), inter-faction surplus trade, relief and reinforcement; fleets carry their provisions. **So base upkeep is already at parity with the hive** (the 2026-10-18 13:35 line above understated it); the one gap is structure upkeep, unpaid on both sides.
- **DECISION 2026-10-18 16:10 (the user, after the hw197 read: "let's do 1 as well"): the swarm softens when it saves marines (1a).** `ThreatGroundFronts.swarmWorth(market)` counts the drop toll: defence points wanted per fleet point lost = `fabricateTroopsPerFP x mult / (frontHoldFraction + dropLossConstant x batteryShare x mult)` (was `/ frontHoldFraction`), the share as worn - so a day of bombardment is worth the marines it spares on the drop too and the swarm lands once the guns are quiet; the strike's three gates read it. 1b (the swarm relieves its own contested fronts, as coalition relief beat it at Mazalot: 18 stand-downs, 381 relief bombard days, 0 of 3 landings won) proposed as the follow-on. Option 3 (base upkeep) found already at parity - nothing built. Trial hw198 (set A).
- **DECISION 2026-10-18 16:20 (the user): tangible feeding both sides - "make the supply thing tangible so they send convoys both sides to maintain worlds and forward bases. The convoys don't need to return home they can just be absorbed when they reach their target".** Built as `ThreatFeeding` (knob `threatinc_feedConvoys`, load 3 months, reserve 1): a world eats from a larder only a sailing fills. Hive: the feed's allotment beyond the world's own forge output docks and sails as a feeding swarm (a Defense Swarm sent as a reinforcement, fuel paid, seated in the target garrison = absorbed, cargo to the larder; lost if killed). Bases: `feedSize` draws the depot alone (no instant home/faction draw), a feeding convoy of 3 months of the bill sails when credit + at sea < 1 month, settled where it lands and expired. Garrison upkeep untouched both sides (the status quo was already equal). Larders open with reserve + 1 months once. -> `hive-stocks-and-upkeep.md` "Tangible feeding", `strategy-convoys.md` "Feeding convoys". First trial: the round after hw198.
- **hw198 read (2026-10-18 16:48, 1a soften): no effect on the drop.** Drops on intact guns 39 / 56 / 33 percent of the Threat's drops vs hw197 35 / 50 / 37; hives 85 / 102 / 124 vs 95 / 88 / 119 (variance). Why: `ThreatStrikeFGI` lands as soon as `readyToLand` holds with the troops aboard - the land-or-bombard worth (`swarmWorth`) only decides whether a SHORT landing keeps bombarding, so it cannot make a swarm that can already hold soften first. A real soften rule gates the landing on the guns themselves (land once gunDefence is under what the toll makes worth bombarding off) - open for the user. Humans eradicated 22 / 11 / 3 on set A (a trends human: hw194 23, hw195 12, hw197 2, hw198 22). -> `game-runs-2.md` s54.
- **Are the humans cheating - resources teleporting, bases cheaper to keep (user, 2026-10-18 17:00; audit on hw198a)?** No instant move found that the Threat lacks: founding, relief, aid and (since 17:00) feeding sail as convoys; link garrison upkeep still draws home / in-reach markets on the spot with a haul fee (hw198a: 87k of 157k supplies) as the hive's navy charge draws the one stock; pooled siege funding relays by convoy. The STRUCTURAL edges are vanilla's: (1) human core colonies pay no size upkeep and come pre-built (industries, stations, batteries, marine production) - hw198a end: 144 colony size-points at 0 vs the hive's 256 at 30k/mo; (2) supplies never bind the humans - stocks GREW while colonies fell (Hegemony 101k -> 214k, four factions ~510k idle at the end) because the hull pool and the planner gate their spending, while the Threat spent 71.5k of a 68.5k income (feed 44 pct) and sat at 8k; (3) "made" supplies tripled as links built producers (Hegemony 14 -> 49 units/mo = 37k, all four ~87k/mo vs the hive's 68.5k) - legal, the hive's forges do the same; (4) reserves COVER vanilla shortages a blockade makes (hw198a: ~344k supplies, ~840k fuel issued as cover) where a blockaded hive's imports are simply cut; (5) mobilisation gifts reserveInitialMonths 6 of banking once, militia 5 marines/size/mo free. Hulls: humans ~31k FP total (yards ~10.9k FP/mo, Hegemony 2.9k -> 15.3k with 12.3k FREE at the end) vs the Threat's 72.8k FP at 9.2k/mo - vanilla patrols (28 vs 20 FP passes) are not it. Levers, the user's call: size upkeep on human colonies too (true parity, turns the hoard into a constraint); nothing else is a cheat. -> `game-runs-2.md` s54.
- **Every jar run on this machine on 2026-10-13 before 20:36 (hw174-hw184) lacked the other machine's four morning commits (hive guns x3, leashed reinforcements, saturation unrest)** - the ground-war reads of s51/s52 are not of the release build; merged a961b808, rerun from pre-opening snapshots as hw185+ (`game-runs-2.md` s53). The user's standing rule: fetch + merge `origin/main` and rebuild before any test session.
- **K2: the founding kit and one prong's trip reserved in the supplies stock ahead of the navy and the fleets away (user, 2026-10-13 ~17:15, after hw177c's K3 read and a Fable review).** The navy fit's want stays one kit a month (user: "One kit a month", not the held sends' kits); K4 (the held foundings' bookings out of the surplus test's demand) after K2 is tested. Retest from hw177c's own saves, then random seeds. `game-runs-2.md` s51. **hw180 (K2, 3 games from hw177c's d2863): unfrozen** - 89 / 71 / 72 hives vs 47, founded 37 / 23 / 23 vs 0; cost 39-75k FP of navy swarms starved for upkeep the reserve left unpaid (recycling them instead proposed). **Then built (Fable's plan, accepted 2026-10-13 ~17:45, f0536aa7):** upkeep kept ahead recycles the smallest swarms into the bank, a hive with navy upkeep unpaid in the last month builds no navy (`ThreatColonyManager.navyUnpaidRecently`), the keptahead line names no hive, and K4 (`ThreatFuel.surplus(c, true)` in `canFound`); hw181 from the same save the trial. **hw181 read:** the churn's cost cut ~4x (builds 71-105k vs 172-189k FP, 0 lost, 40-88k recycled at the hull share), hives 81 / 79 / 76 vs 89 / 71 / 72, founded the same; a rotation across hives remains (one stock: one builds while another is unpaid); K4 idle here (seedings held on the stock itself, not the surplus arm). `game-runs-2.md` s51.
- **POST-0.8 FLAG: fuel never sends a fleet home (user, 2026-10-12, "flag it for post 0.8").** Asked whether fleets run out of fuel and leave as they do on supplies: no. Supplies send fleets home (the hold, `fleet-provisions.md`); fuel is drawn at launch for the passage there and back, nothing burns it in flight, and only bombardment spends it - carried fuel first, then the supply line's spendable reserve (`ThreatGroundFronts.payOrdnance`, `ordnanceSources`), a relief never below its leg home (`reliefFuelHome`). Dry, the slices stop and the fleet holds the orbit on supplies alone. The swarm now carries a 10-day bombard hold (the line above, same evening) and a dry Defend stands down home; the human side still holds on silent guns. The asymmetry to settle after 0.8: a human siege can pour everything and still hold indefinitely with silent guns - whether bombardment should eat the leg home or the hold be fuel-bound like supplies. No change before 0.8. -> memory `fuel-hold-after-0-8`
- **A swarm fleet carries its bombard ordnance, 10 days of it (user, 2026-10-12 evening, "the swarm pays nothing for fuel and they could keep bombarding", chose "Carry a bombard hold", "10 days").** The stock had paid 61k fuel over 18 days of bombardment for a hive whose port was offline and imports cut, where a human fleet runs dry. Now `threatinc_swarmBombardHoldDays` (10) x `threatinc_swarmBombardFuelPerFPDay` (2.86) x FP is a swarm fleet's hold (`ThreatGroundFronts.bombardHoldFull`, fleet memory `MEM_BOMBARD_HOLD`), drawn from the stock at first use wherever it stands (the load it sailed with) and after that refilled only within hold range of a hive whose port stands, undisrupted, with imports not fully cut (`portArms`, `reloadBombardHold`). Over its own hive the swarm bombards with its armed fleets only (`tickSwarmBombard`, log "N FP armed of M over the orbit ... F aboard"); a dry garrison or reinforcement over a hive that cannot arm it flies to the nearest hive that can, reloads and comes back (`ThreatColonyManager.sendToRearm`, `rearmAndReturn`, passage paid both ways, logs "Swarm rearms: ... leaves X for Y"); a dry strike or Defend stands down home as before (`ordnanceAvailable`, `orbitDoneFor`). Knob 0 restores the stock paying every day. -> `ground-war-orbit-control.md` "The swarm holds the orbit"
- **The swarm bombards in proportion to its weight, and pays fuel for it (user, 2026-10-12, "1% for how much fuel? ... Shouldnt be proportional to the FP overhead?").** `tickSwarmBombard` had no visual, no log line and no fuel, and `swarmBombardPer30Days` capped the grind at `swarmFrontBombardPer30Days` (0.6) of the front per 30 days x FP / (FP + the front's defence figure) - 1,015 FP over Delta Mengryla I read about 1% a day against a 1,378-marine push on a starved size-3 hive (counter-attack 253 d: fed 17%, the 0.25 pace floor), so the swarm's weight bought nothing past the front's figure and the hive fell. Now: marines a day = `swarmBombardMarinesPerFPDay` (0.05) x FP over the orbit / the front's cover (`coverMult`, dug in up to 1.5x; a pushing front is exposed) x veterancy (1,015 FP: 51 a day), fuel `swarmBombardFuelPerFPDay` (2.86, the human saturation rate) x FP a day from the swarm's stock (`ThreatFuel`, purpose "bombard"), a day the stock cannot pay skipped (`ThreatFuel.held`, log "Swarm bombardment over X held: no fuel"); `swarmBombardMarinesPerFPDay` 0 falls back to the fraction rule. Vanilla's bombard burst on the planet and a log line once a whole day ("Swarm bombards the <owner> front at X: N FP over the orbit, M marines a day, front K marines, held D d, fuel F a day"); the board's row tooltip quotes the marines a day. The relief's bombard of a Threat army (`reliefBombardPer30Days`) keeps the old shape. Compiled 18:17, jar NOT written (game running), untested. -> `ground-war-orbit-control.md` "The swarm holds the orbit"
- **hw161 (2026-10-12, random seeds on the 18:21 jar): the proportional bombardment drank ZhiFarah's founding fund** - 1.12M fuel over the run, stock under 1k from w3,300, founded 70 vs hw160b's 135, hives 41 vs 119; a paid 571k and still expanded (76 hives). Fix proposed: the bombardment pays from the founding/fund budget (S6), not the raw stock. `game-runs-2.md` s44.
- **hw161: engage-on-arrival feeds reinforcements into the daily siege grinder** - 1,287-2,420 outweighed engagements a game at a median 6-16% of the hostile weight, daily-siege losses 4x hw160b (226k vs 58k FP), b's hives flat under refills. Fix proposed: engage only while the player holds station (`ThreatBlockade.playerHoldsStation`); NPC-siege arrivals seated as before; the feed under a siege gated by the enough rule (`ThreatColonyManager.engageOnArrival`). `game-runs-2.md` s44.
- **The fuel census cannot show the bombard spend (hw161):** `ThreatFuel.monthSummary` brackets purposes for supplies only and `PURPOSES` lacks "bombard"; sum the `Swarm bombards the ... fuel F a day` lines instead (`reads/check-orbit.sh`).
- **DECISIONS 2026-10-12 20:10 (user, after hw161: "both fixes"):** (1) a reinforcement engages on arrival only while the player holds station (`ThreatBlockade.playerHoldsStation`); an NPC-siege arrival is seated as before (`ThreatColonyManager.engageOnArrival` call site). (2) The ordnance has a founding budget: while a seeding is held on fuel this month (`ThreatFuel.heldOnFuelThisMonth`), a hold load or a stock-paid bombard day leaves one founding's fuel in the stock (`ThreatGroundFronts.bombardReserve`, the S6 rule for fuel; knob `threatinc_foundingBudget`). (3) The census brackets fuel by purpose too (`ThreatFuel.ALL_PURPOSES`: "bombard", "bombard hold"). Built jar 20:17, trial hw162 from hw161's pre-war snapshots (a d1,927, b d2,202, c d2,186 - the user: "skip the seed phase and jump right to first strike").

Built for hw163.
- **hw162 end (2026-10-12 21:00): all three the swarm's** - at hw161's clock a 70 / b 78 / c 192 hives (hw161 76 / 41 / 130; hw160 43 / 130 / 149), engagements 0; b's bombardment still fires 1.63M fuel (821 days) and its stock sits at 0-27k; the daily siege losses (b 202k vs hw160b 58k) are the 2026-10-12 human build's heavier, twice-as-frequent sieges (attacker mean 553 -> 1,183 FP, launches 955 -> 2,395), not the arrivals. `game-runs-2.md` s45. hw163 = the hold refund from the same snapshots.
- **hw163 (the hold refund, same snapshots, 21:00-21:49): refunds 389k / 670k / 634k fuel back, but hives 62 / 43 / 108 (hw162 70 / 79 / 208) with c besieged 14x more by the humans on the same seed - chaotic divergence, not the refund. RULE from here: a fix is read on two or more games a snapshot (hw164 = hw163 repeated to size the variance). `game-runs-2.md` s46.
- **hw164 (hw163 repeated unchanged, 21:53-22:38): a 54 / b 118 / c 149 at hw161's clock (hw163 62 / 52 / 107)** - b 43 vs 131 on one jar and snapshot: the noise between two games is as large as every build-to-build difference of the day; hw163 was noise, the refund jar stands; three games of one snapshot read a 70 / 62 / 54, b 78 / 43 / 131, c 192 / 108 / 169. `game-runs-2.md` s47.
- **Swarm reinforcements fight for the orbit on arrival, and lift no blockade until they have fought (user, 2026-10-12, holding orbit over Delta Mengryla I: "the threat just reinforced their defenders and there was no intel notification ... they didnt attack my fleet over the planet until after they did this").** The log: three Defense Swarms of 395 / 340 / 332 FP sent from Golter 15 ly away as regional relief sailed blinkered (`FLEET_IGNORES_OTHER_FLEETS`) through the player's fleet, were seated as garrison at 700 units, and the blockade read "imports cut 50% (739 over 735)" then "0% (635 over 1,049)" the moment the second was seated - the batteries' machinery and metals came back and the ground figure stepped up before a shot was fired; the garrison mustered 709 FP a tick later. No ground action happened (no counter-attack, no stratum retaken; fleets never feed a hive's ground figure). Now `ThreatColonyManager.checkReinforcementArrivals` reads hostiles over the target within `ORBIT_HOLD_RANGE` (1,500): with any, `engageOnArrival` takes the blinders off, sets the swarm aggressive on an aggressive orbit, marks it engaged and unfought, and leaves it on the in-transit list - nothing is seated while an enemy holds station; the marks clear when the orbit reads no hostiles. `ThreatBlockade.hiveCut`, while the player's fleet is within hold range (`playerHoldsStation`), weighs only swarms without the unfought mark (`foughtPointsNear`); `FoughtMark`, a fleet listener, clears the mark on the swarm's first battle; off-screen orbits weigh as before. Not built (declined): a "Swarm Reinforced" notice, gating the hive's counter-attack on the orbit. Jar 2026-10-12, untested. -> `swarm-defence.md` "Fight on arrival", `ground-war-defenders.md` "Blockade"
- **One fleet inside the radius no longer stops the swarm bombarding an enemy front on its hive (user, 2026-10-12, the last fleet in a system the swarm held: "my presence shouldn't necessarily stop them").** `swarmOrbitContested` once read any armed hostile fleet within 1,500 units, so a lone fleet dancing there kept `tickSwarmBombard` off while the hive's last stratum fell. Now the hostiles within range must weigh `orbitContestFraction` (0.5) of `swarmOrbitStrength`, the colony theatre's own rule (`orbitHeldAgainst`) mirrored; with no swarm there any armed fleet still contests. The unspawned flotilla's `coverFP` keeps its old test (at least the swarm's FP). Jar 2026-10-12 15:49, untested. -> `ground-war-orbit-control.md` "The swarm holds the orbit"
- **A hive's siege column counts what stands; a colony's keeps Held (user, 2026-10-11 21:10: "noticed sieges still say like Held 0/4 for size 4 colony, and then when the colony drops in size due to siege it says held 0/3 ... legacy artefact", then "do recommended but not labelled stands maybe Strata ... for threat sieges against humans we keep as before").** The fronts table's column is **Strata** on hives: the strata the hive still stands on (size less strata held), down by a stratum taken or a size starved, up only on a counter-attack retake, the hive falls at 0; the row tooltip gives "Stands on N of S strata, K starved away since the landing" (`GroundFront.sizeAtLanding`, 0 on older saves). A table holding a colony's front is headed **Held** and a colony's cell stays `held/size` districts (`ThreatWarBoard.heldCell`, `heldLabel`). And every level saturation razes off a human colony posts a "Level Razed" notice with the size before and after (`ThreatRazing.levelRazed`; the player's own razing is silent) - a loss for good, not siege progress. Jar 21:20, untested. -> `war-board.md` fronts table
- **Staged guards sail themselves, one a fleet (user, 2026-10-11 19:35: "when fleets are staged over haven and i order them somewhere, they suddenly all disappear then respawn coming out of the planet. so rather than staging fleet being used the game deletes them then respawns them").** `ThreatFleetOrders.buildPlayerForce` builds fleets from the colony's own free hulls first; past them each task force on station is re-tasked in place (`ThreatFleetOrders.retask`: its guard order ends, what it carries settles at the base, own hulls top it up when short of the ask, it is provisioned for the trip as a built fleet would be - the draw is the difference - and sails on the order's assignments; refused, it stands as before). Until 19:40 `ThreatAidCapacity.foldStaged` expired each folded guard and the yards built its points again, and a fleet could fold two guards (19:30: a Med hunt of 1,451 built five fleets and folded nine; `foldFit` removed with the fold). Sieges and bombings take them the same way since 20:00 (`ThreatPurgeFGI.takeStaged`: the guards stay in orbit, alive, until the expedition spawns - at once while the player is in spawn range, as at the board over the base - and are handed to it where they float, the built fleets appearing beside them with no incremental spawn (`spawnBuiltAndStaged`); the first cut of 20:00 stashed their ships at the click and let vanilla respawn them one a quarter day, the user at 20:10: "the guard fleets in orbit still got deleted then respawned ... dont just fucking delete them"); their ledger entries ride the group as a fold's did. Only an expedition the player cannot see takes them off the map, when its route leaves the base (`departStaged`): their ships are kept and put back together, the same hulls, as an AI fleet with its transponder on, should it spawn later (a player-mode fleet showed as "Unidentified Fleet, Unknown"). An expedition ending before it sails hands them back as guards (`releaseStagedLive`). Jar 20:20, untested. -> `hull-pool.md` 4, `strategy-orbit-outposts.md` "A guard over your own colony is staging"
- **The player's Hunt clears the whole system or serves its term, and one press speaks once (user, 2026-10-11 20:10, five "Swarms Hunted Down" from one Hunt while swarms were still in the system: "fleets must have been there like 3 days and theres still swarms left but fuck all. why are they running?").** The done-check (`ThreatSoftening.advanceSingle`) read only the swarms ON an orbit (`weakest` over `ThreatIntel.worldFP`, the 1,500-unit `ORBIT_HOLD_RANGE`): the survivors of the big fight fled the orbit, every world read zero, and the hunt went home with the "loose" swarms (`Report.looseFP`) alive. Now a player hunt in its system with every orbit clear chases the nearest loose swarm (`nearestLoose`, `ThreatFleetOrders.chaseLoose`: INTERCEPT for the rest of the term, `Order.chasingId`); when that one is dead, gone or back on an orbit it re-judges; en route it flies on while the report holds loose FP; it goes home only when the system reads nothing at all, or its term ends, or it is badly hurt. NPC hunts keep the orbit-only rule (their job is the siege's orbit gate). The spam: Med/Max build several fleets with one order each and each spoke; now one press shares `Order.groupId` and `ThreatSoftening.speaks` lets the first fleet say "Swarms Hunted Down" / "Hunt Moves On", the siblings stay silent (`forgetGroup` drops the keys when the last stands down). Compiled, jar NOT rebuilt (game running), untested. -> `strategy-hunting-reach.md` "The PLAYER'S HUNT"
- **A fleet back on station is restocked the moment it settles (user, 2026-10-11 19:30, two guards at Haven reading "0 d supplies" for days: "0 days of supplies is something supposed to happen? they just sit there").** `ThreatReturns.settle` empties the hold (its supplies go to the host) and the next `ThreatUpkeep` pass, up to 5 days later, refilled it; now `ThreatFleetOrders.restation` (both overloads) calls `ThreatUpkeep.restockFleet` at once. `ThreatReturns.holdFull`'s fallback for a never-loaded fleet is the stay its distance from home says (30 days in-system, `ThreatReach.sortieStay`), not 90. The fleets-table cell is "79 d aboard" ("79 d supplies" was cut off). Jar 19:40, untested. -> `fleet-provisions.md` rule 1
- **A fleet re-tasked pays only the difference: in-system orders carry 30 days, and the forces folding into a sortie bring their provisions (user, 2026-10-11 19:05, at a same-system guard quoting 12,195 supplies: "why does it take so many supplies to fly from one planet to another in the same fucking system" ... "if i provision 30 days of supplies for a fleet to sit in system. if i move it to another planet in the system. i dont pay another 30 days of supplies. and if i send it out of system, i pay the difference of required vs what is currently carried").** The 12,195 was no flight cost (0 ly, 0 fuel) but the hull deposit (30 per 25 fleet points, back on return) plus 90 days of the ships' upkeep as the hold. Now: `ThreatReach.sortieStay(ly)` is `threatinc_sortieHomeStayDays` (30) for a destination in the base's own system, where the fleet restocks from the base when low, `sortieStayDays` (90) beyond - both sides, NPC navies the same; and a staged force settles at the base as it is re-tasked, ahead of its own draw (`ThreatFleetOrders.retask`), its fuel, deposit and hold back in the reserve where the draw finds them, so the quote, the gate and the draw are need less carried (`foldedProvisions` / `ThreatReturns.carried`; the prompt's second line "The fleets at Haven already carry 14,219 supplies" - plain words and no zero figures, the user at 19:20: "dont say things like folding in its fucking jargon. just say currently carry"; a guard built at its own base drew no fuel, a trip inside one system burns none, as in vanilla). Jar 19:06, untested. -> `fleet-provisions.md` rule 1
- **Player fleets take the NPC hold trim: the draw never exceeds the quoted figure (user, 2026-10-11 18:50, "well the figure matches npc navies right. they all calculate supplies required before they are sent out", then "Same as NPC").** The quote is the NPC gate's price (`ThreatFleetOrders.sortieWants` = `forceProvisions`); the built hulls can want a hold a few percent over it, so a player fleet now does what an NPC fleet does in `buildTaskForce`: leaves its smallest warships home until the paid hold feeds the rest (`TRIM_FLOOR` 0.75), and is held - "Order Refused: the hulls built at Haven want more supplies than it can hold them for" - past that. Both under `threatinc_stagedLaunch`. Jar 18:42, untested. -> `fleet-provisions.md` rules 1 and 7
- **Every player order says what fuel and supplies it takes, and is greyed out when its base cannot pay (user, 2026-10-11 18:45: "sieges hunts and bombards etc, they require supplies and fuel right. bombard seems to say fuel but others dont? needs to say exactly how many supplies fuel etc is required, and of course if not enough then button is greyed out").** A player force's draw is `ThreatFleetOrders.forceProvisions` (the voyage deposit `ReachRules.voyageCost` plus the hold `ThreatReach.sortieHold`, priced per point before the hulls are built - the built hulls' own hold can run a few percent over); `provisionsShort` is the gate, read by `ThreatAid.quoteTaskForce` (Fleet, Defend, Hunt, Support, aid Defend and Hunt buttons and their tooltips: "Haven has 1,200 fuel and 8,440 supplies / The force takes 4,883 fuel and 1,250 supplies"), by every prompt ("Takes N fuel and M supplies from Haven's F fuel and S supplies", `ThreatFactionView.addTakesLine`) and again by `buildPlayerForce` ("Order Refused"). Siege: `siegeBlockFacts` gates the player's on `expeditionWants` fuel and supplies, its prompt carries the line; Bombard: `bombardBlockFacts` adds supplies to its fuel gate, its prompt a supplies line. Replaces fleet-provisions rule 1's "a player task force always sails". The Support / Defend gate and the fronts table's buttons now read the Force tier the order sails at (`orbitRefusal(..., tier)`), not tier 0. Jar 18:38, untested. -> `fleet-provisions.md` rule 1, `war-board.md` ladders

Staging is only ever an explicit Fleet order. Yellow in the Hulls cell = only a reduced task force (`aidGuardMinFP`) can sail, white a full one, red none (`ThreatFactionView.fleetColor`). - **Why did hw143b hold a prong at Gilead refused every month before the war opened (2026-10-10 16:55)?** `ThreatOffensive.pass` priced prongs with `bankLimit` set, which skips the opening bar in `IncursionManager.stagedPlan` (`strikeStagedOpenFP`); the prong was refused on its day by the bar and its 8k bill, held out of the fund, made the bar read 8k higher (14-24k of 40k shown). Fixed the same day: the pass prices nothing while no faction is at war and the fund is under the bar. -> `strikes-staged.md`
- **Why were four of hw175b's six opening prongs refused (2026-10-13 12:52)?** The other half of hw143b's hole: the fund held ~50k (over the 40k bar), so `ThreatOffensive.pass` priced a campaign of 6 prongs, deducting each held prong's bill from the fund; every prong launches through `launchStrike` -> `IncursionManager.stagedPlan` with `bankLimit` NaN while `openingWaits()` is still true (nothing has sailed), so the bar read the fund net of its siblings' bills and refused the "now" prong inside the pass at 9,171 (silently - the pass logs only its summary, "0 strike(s) now and 5 to follow, of 6 planned"; the one "waits to open the war" line is it) and the first three held prongs at ~17k / 26k / 34k; Tigra City (~43k) and Yama sailed - the coordinated opening split. Held-prong refusals: 1 of 48 games (hw163a's one refusal was a fuel gate after the war opened, not this). **The silent now-prong drop is common: 13 of 51 games hw160-hw176** (hw160a/b, hw161a-hw164a, hw166b, hw167b, hw168c, hw169a, hw174a, hw175b, hw176b - first line "0 strike(s) now and N to follow, of M planned" next to "waits to open the war: the strike fund holds 31-40k of 40000"): the held prong's bill takes the fund under the bar, the now-prong is refused, the war opens with one prong of two (8k instead of 16k) - every opening since the 40k bar (2026-10-10) was at risk. **Fixed** (user-approved after a Fable review, 2026-10-13): the bar skips a prong of a priced campaign (`pricedFP` set - both the pass's now-prong and `ThreatOffensive.poll` set it; the campaign is priced only when the fund met the bar). First run hw177: every opening sailed whole. Its kin, the fuel gate: a priced prong's fuel came back on its day at the priced size, but `stagedPlan` gated the passage of the day's (bigger) plan against a stock others had drawn (hw177b refused short by 118 fuel, hw176a, hw163a); since jar 2026-10-13 ~16:00 the gate checks the passage of min(plan, `pricedFP`) and `launchStrike` pays min(stock, passage) - a drained stock still holds the prong. First run hw179. -> `strikes-staged.md`
- **hw154 (random seeds from day 0, one sector a game, 2026-10-11): b ZhiFarah 71 hives / 21 colonies, c AphelionDysnomia 100 / 20 - the swarm's; a OceanPena 13 of a 44 peak / 34, lost late (w2,880 on). Fable review: the fund sat at 48-62k through the fall and the exchange stayed positive; hives died on the ground - 4-7k flotillas from 61 forward bases landed on hives holding ~1k (the posture share), the rally declined ("1087 could stand against 4431"), the hold paid only the posture's need (`ThreatPosture.needFP`) in full. Nothing turns a 50k fund into a 4.5k stand at the hive being landed on. 35 of 56 landings eradicated the hive.** Test bed `save_OceanPena_...hw154ad2538`. -> `game-runs-2.md` hw154
- **hw160 (random seeds from day 0 on the staged-launch build, 2026-10-11 16:34-17:19): a OceanPena 44 hives at the end = its peak / 34 colonies (hw154a 13 of 44) - contested, 45 hives eradicated on the ground w2,400-3,700 but 41 founded; b ZhiFarah 119 / 16 (hw154b 71 / 21); c AphelionDysnomia 148 / 4 (hw154c 100 / 20). All three better than hw154, no exception; fuel idles 1-1.5M with the navy supplies-bound everywhere.** -> `game-runs-2.md` hw160
- **Approaches first and the base one size up (user, 2026-10-11, after the hw154a review: "I would expect the swarm to already attack all the closest bases to its world ... proximity bases should be highest priority regardless of size ... the force sent should be measured as the FB size + 1 unless it is at size 4 or more").** Forward bases within `threatinc_offensiveApproachLY` (10) of their staging hive are priced first whatever the focus (`ThreatOffensive.pass`); a strike at a base under size 4 is sized for the base one size up - the next station tier (`IncursionManager.sizedDefence`, knob `threatinc_strikeBaseGrowth`). Built; hw155 (12:19-12:44, three games from hw154a's d2538 snapshot): a 36 / b 20 / c 38 hives at d3,800 against hw154a's 17 - c held with a 49k navy, a held its hives but bled 50k -> 21k of garrisons in the last 150 days of daily siege fights (exchange still positive), b fell the same way (its exchange went negative); base strikes 15-20 a game killing 53-67% (hw154a 13, 31%); approaches priced 3-35k by the faction's seen navy, one a pass, the fund at 200-450 after; humans founded ~6 bases a kill. -> `strikes-staged.md` 8
- **Founding convoys (user, 2026-10-11, after the hw155 read: "make humans need physical convoys to found a FB like the threat has a seeding swarm").** A forward base's kit (5,500 supplies + 800 fuel, `ThreatOutposts.linkCost`) sails from the paying base in a real convoy (`ThreatConvoys.dispatchFounding`, escort and passage paid from the stock beside the kit) and the base is founded when it lands with the whole kit (`pollFounding`, `ThreatFrontlines.foundLanded`); lost at sea, the kit is lost; a site taken meanwhile turns it home; one kit a hive at a time. Knob `threatinc_foundingConvoy`. Context: hw155 founded 58-67 bases a game on the spot, ~6 per base killed, with the humans holding 50-330k supplies - the price was real but instant and uninterceptable, the Seeding Swarm sails. First trial hw156 (two copies of hw154a's d2538 + Aphelion's pre-mobilisation d2229). -> `frontlines.md` "Founding convoy"
- **Staged launches (user, 2026-10-11 14:20, after hw156 / gs1 on the carried-provisions build: "they should never run out unless unforeseen circumstances ... raids should never launch with zero supplies for FB, FB needs to be supplied/staged with supplies and fuel first").** An NPC task force, hunt or raid sails only with its full fuel, deposit and hold from the base it launches at, else the draw goes back and it stays home (`ThreatFleetOrders.buildTaskForce`, log `Order held at`); no market pays toward another base's fleet remotely (`ThreatSoftening.huntDonors` empty, so `payableFP` sizes on the base's own stock) - convoys stage the base (`ThreatConvoys.stagingTargets`). Cause fixed: gs1a drew 105 of 838 orders with `hold 0 of N` from empty forward bases (sized pooled, drawn local - the founding-convoy bug's class) and 105 of 207 stand-downs had 0 aboard. CONFIRMED hw159 (15:46): draws 709 / 925 / 740, zero-hold 0, trimmed 236 / 225 / 73 sailed, stand-downs 6 / 9 / 24 all relief rotations; hives 35 / 43 / 99. Knob `threatinc_stagedLaunch`; the player always sails; sieges still pool remotely (`IncursionManager.siegeDonors`) - the user's next call. Doc `fleet-provisions.md` rule 7. Also rec 2 of `handover-2026-10-11-gs1.md`: `ThreatRaiders` margin a knob, `threatinc_raiderMargin` 3 (was 1.5: 0 of 135 founding convoys died in hw156).
- **Why do founding convoys arrive "short" with every supply aboard (gs1, 2026-10-11)?** Vanilla's engine burns the convoy's cargo fuel as it sails - 55-128 of the kit's 800 on 3-9 ly - after `dispatchFounding` already paid the passage from the stock; the whole-kit test sent 15 of 28 convoys home at mid-run. A fight takes supplies before fuel (`ThreatConvoys.trimToHulls`), so `pollFounding` now calls a convoy short only on supplies and founds on the fuel aboard (kit + passage paid once).
- **hw146 (the fixed hold, from ck1 to d3,800; 2026-10-10 19:41): 53 / 45 / 66 hives**, humans 24 / 31 / 20 - no game lost by 3,800; the hold paid 64k FP to b's hives yet b slid to 1.07k/hive with 45k idle in the fund (the hold pays `needFP`, which the fights do not confirm) - the first Fable question. -> `game-runs-2.md` hw146, handover-2026-10-10-evening 5
- **hw148 (third seed ZhiFarah from day 0, build a68a032f; 2026-10-10 20:53): 41 / 42 / 43 hives**, humans 20 / 25 / 29, navies 100-111k - no game lost by 3,800, the war opened at d2,213-2,422; b and c are 10-12 hives off their peaks and falling at the end while the fund idles on 66-96k FP reading "0 short" (hw146b's pattern again) - the needFP question is the open one (`game-runs-2.md` hw148, handover evening section 9).
- **Why do the frontier hives fall while the navy and fund are full (hw148b/c, read 2026-10-10 21:05)?** They die on the ground: a siege of 0.9-1.9k FP (median by faction) lands on a size-2/5 hive holding vanilla's standing patrols (76-1,200 FP, `ThreatPosture.minimumFP`), since an NPC siege turns home only at `siegeBreakOffRatio` 1.0 x its FP in orbit; 77 of 78 of b's late fights had the defender below the siege, 17 landings, 19 cores destroyed in 300 days, though the exchange ran 1.5:1 the swarm's way. The posture's need rises only once the force is read and help comes behind the landing, so the hold's `needFP` reads "0 short" with 66-96k idle in the fund and 33k garrison idle in three quiet systems at pressure 0. Options S1-S3 in handover evening 9a (S1: exposed hives want the largest siege seen x the break-off ratio).
- **The line (S1, built 2026-10-10 21:10 by the user's loop "try different strategies until threat win"; `threatinc_postureLine`).** An exposed hive needs at least the strongest faction's median siege seen in a year x `siegeBreakOffRatio` x `postureMargin` x its exposure (`ThreatSwarmIntel.siegeLineFP`, set in the posture pass), so the fund, a recall and the forge hold what turns a siege home before it lands. First trial hw150 from hw148b/c's snapshots. -> `hive-garrison-and-upkeep.md` "The line".
- **The line refuted (hw150, 2026-10-10 21:38; the A/B on hw149's saves): b 35 hives against 71, c 31 against 33.** The raised frontier wants (~3k FP a colony) pushed supplies spend to 93-105k a month against 84-86k income, the stock was 0 in four months, the forges could not keep the fund's 13k / 146k FP (c's defender still below the siege in 266 of 275 fights), landings fell a little (17 / 20 against 27 / 22) and the spread died (b founded 3 against 43). Supplies bind, not FP: `threatinc_postureLine` false (code kept). What turned hw149b round was its supplies surplus spent on 43 foundings; c, at 0-15k supplies on a 125k navy, founded nothing in either build. -> handover evening 9c.
- **A frozen navy is a bug; S6 approved (user, 2026-10-10 21:45: "while not lost a frozen navy i would consider a bug which means fix and reuse that seed to test. S6 sounds fine").** hw149c/hw150c - a 125-148k navy on 0 supplies founding nothing for 1,200 days - is a defect, not a loss: the fit must leave a founding budget (S6) before it keeps a navy. Built from c's snapshot `save_ZhiFarah_3202965389391319559hw148cd3620` and tested there until the game turns, then back to random seeds.
- **Why did hw149c found nothing with a 125k navy (read 2026-10-10 21:50)?** 43 seedings were held on supplies (`ThreatFuel.canFound` wants one founding kit free in stock) while the stock sat at 0-2,600: of a 98k income the feed took 41-43k (sustenance and 130 size-ups), the fleets away 38k, the navy charge 17k, build 3k - growth drew the free stock every poll before a kit could bank (`ThreatColonyUpkeep.feed`). hw149b's stock reached 167k and it founded 43. S6 built: growth leaves one kit while a seeding is held (`threatinc_foundingBudget`) -> `hive-stocks-and-upkeep.md` "A founding budget before growth".
