- **DECISION 2026-10-19 15:30 (the user: "s1") - S1 BUILT (jar 15:37, `patch-s1.pl`): STAGING IS THE BASE'S COMMITMENT.** (1) A hammer that ends without its siege sailing (failure, neutral, worlds taken) leaves its staging to the base as a commitment (`ThreatConvoys.PlayStaging.committed`, key `base#commit`, the latest replacing an older one; a live play staging there takes it over) - so `ownKeep` still holds the landing's stock and the planner ships none of it to another base. Cleared when a siege sails from the base (`ThreatConvoys.clearCommitment` at IncursionManager's expedition draw) or after `threatinc_stagingCommitDays` 180 with no hammer re-staged there (lapses in `siegeStock`). Log: `Staging: X keeps its commitment to the landing (...) after play P`, `X met its commitment - a siege sailed`, `X's commitment lapsed`. (2) THE HAMMER WAITS WHILE STOCK RISES (`threatinc_councilWaitWhileRising`): `siegeCanPay` sets `lastRefusalGate / Have / Need`; at the end of a 30-day wait window `ThreatPlays.strike` fails only if the refused figure has not risen by `WAIT_RISE` 0.1 of the need since the window opened and the gate is the same (`stockRising`; `Play.refusedGate / refusedHave`); log `stock rising (...), waits another 30 d`. (3) A FEW STAGING HAMMERS A FACTION: the invade pass keeps `threatinc_invadeStagingMax` 2 staging at once (`stagingHammers`), one a base (`stagingHammerAt`), replacing my per-faction (14:05) and per-base (14:50) cuts. hw228 (A, convoys only without S1) killed at d~600 for the jar; trial hw229 (B) / hw230 (A) from 15:37 (run-siege-28). The humans had the same shape with two holes: (1) fuel and supplies shortage read only what WAS drawn (ThreatFactionStock.noteDemand) while hulls and marines noted every refusal (noteHullShort / noteMarineShort) - a relief provisioned at 1 FP registered 1 FP, so the Persean League with 17,461 FP of free hulls and dry bases never read short and answerShortage fired 0 times in hw244a; (2) the surplus half (surplusProducer / mayConvert) had no human caller, and idle hulls were never recycled while their standing upkeep ate the supplies. THE CONVERSION `ThreatCoreBuild.convertStep` (the swarm's convertSurplus for the humans, knob `threatinc_convertSurplusHuman`): shortest = supplies or hulls, no core colony with a slot for their producer -> a Fuel Production the fuel surplus spares (`surplusProducer`, no special item / AI core, no front) on the largest colony that can import a yard's inputs is torn down and Heavy Industry started or its price held; one a faction a 30 d (`mayConvert`); never a yard. RULE FOR TRIALS: before flipping a knob in settings.json, `grep <key> data/config/LunaSettings.csv` - a hit means the trial flips the store (`perl -i -pe` on threatinc.json.data while no game runs; the game rewrites that file at every launch from its own values) and the first game's log is checked for the effect before the round is trusted.

- **Why nothing musters for Sindria in the user's save (2026-10-22, save of 10-17 run on the 0.8.2 jar):** the garrison (ground defence 14 with 6 of 7 districts gone, 217 armed Regular marines) loses a counter-attack to the 245-troop front the swarm tops up to repel with margin, so `tickDefenderStance` digs in; the 200 marines issued wait 30 d off the blockade (`Convoy holding off Sindria`); relief must outweigh 1,651 FP of swarm and the Diktat has 0 free hulls of 864 at 0 FP/mo of yards (Sindria's Orbital Works suppressed by the front, Cruor's and Volturn's Heavy Industry still building) - the siege-stock chest keeps only hulls the yards make, so it adds nothing; the coalition (League 412 from Mazalot + one partner + the player's 549 FP of guards) fields 1,321 and holds under one-body. Volturn, with no swarm over it, got a 1,070 FP relief the same run. The last district falls in ~2-3 weeks at the push pace unless the orbit is thinned below ~1,320 FP. **Why no hull contract and no allied hull convoy for the Diktat (same save):** a help request for ship hulls posts only at the faction's hull port (`ThreatHulls.hullStatus`), and `ThreatCoalition.aidHulls` lands allied hulls there or returns silently; `hullPort` was the yard making the most ship units else the market with the most standing patrols, and the Diktat had neither (works and High Command suppressed, Cruor's and Volturn's yards building, patrols 0) - hull debt 621 FP, 7 units short, nobody asked. Expedition hulls are the need (`playerExpeditionSizes` at Min). The colonies table's Amount ladder still sails one fleet / half / all (`forceFP`) - the user named only the aggressive actions. - **DECISION 2026-10-24 22:36 (the user, in orbit over Beta Prisma I, size 2, 800 defence, every structure disrupted, imports cut 100%: "ive been sat bombing the colony and its defenses are fine, its growth is halted but not shrinking, and i cant even aford to siege it when its size 2. shortages seem to have no effect").** Three readings, two fixes (jar 22:36, untested). (1) **The 800 is the size anchor, not guns**: `hiveDefensePerSize` 400 x 2 strata ("Deep fabrication strata", `SwarmNexus.apply`, applied while disrupted), which bombing never wears - only a ground front strips strata (facts 194, 377); the bombardment did its work (Core, port and every structure down 20 days). Whether 400 a size is right for a size-2 hive is a balance question left to the user. (2) **Shortages had no effect because a size-2 seed could not starve**: `ThreatColonyUpkeep.feed` floored a seed's fed share at break-even ("unfed it holds, fed it grows") whatever its imports; now a seed is fed only the share of its imports that gets through (holding share capped at 1 - `importCut`), so a full blockade starves it to size 1 at `starveDaysPerSize` (its tooltip reads "Growth: starving, a size in N days"); knob `threatinc_blockadeStarvesSeeds` (default true). Size 1 never shrinks and a hive still dies only to troops (facts 194). (3) **The siege wanted 2,666 marines for a landing of 1,333**: `siegeMarineHeadroom` 2.0 was charged flat, for hulls the orbit costs, over a world with no Defense Swarm and no batteries. Now `IncursionManager.marineHeadroom`: the full knob while the besieger's report (`ThreatIntel.worldFP`) shows a swarm over a world landed on, else 1 / (fleet points left after the guns' planned toll, `bombardPlan`[2]) clamped to [1, knob] - 1 over a gunless world; `siegeRaidStrNeeded(targets, observer)` / `(targets, rate, observer)` and `needAndWear(targets, rate, observer)` carry the sizing faction (null: the full headroom - `siegeFleetSizes` and `siegeWearFP`, which have no faction, so the flotilla is still grown for the worst). Passed from the player tiers (`siegeTierMarines`: Factions.PLAYER), `expeditionWants` (the base's faction), the launch, the per-target pass, `easiestFirst`, `ThreatPlays.payingBase` and `ThreatAttackPlanner`. An unseen swarm now means a short landing - the Scout button is the answer. - **DECISION 2026-10-24 22:55 (the user: "hive should shrink to zero and be destroyed if it reaches 0").** A starving hive no longer stops at size 1: a size-1 seed with its imports cut is fed min(break-even, 1 - `importCut`) like a size-2 one (`ThreatColonyUpkeep.feed`), and when its starvation crosses the shrink floor `ThreatColonyManager.starveOut` runs - a front standing on it takes the ground victory (`ThreatGroundFronts.groundVictory`), else "Hive Starved / The strata are cold" and `eradicate`. The vitality tooltip reads "Growth: starving, destroyed in N days" at size 1 (`daysToLoseSize` no longer MAX at size 1). So a hive now dies two ways: troops, or starved past size 1 under a blockade - AMENDS fact 194 and the 22:36 entry (2). Same knob `blockadeStarvesSeeds`; a forge world below size 3 still grows free (its own output). Jar 22:48, untested.
- **DECISION 2026-10-24 23:16 (the user: "change of direction. Bombardment eliminates chunks of strata. Strata grows over time if colony is fed and the colony increases size when it reaches the next 400 increment. It also decays when unfed.").** THE STRATA: a hive's one figure, `ThreatStrata` (market memory `$threatinc_strata`; an older save starts at size x 400 plus the growth clock's banked share). `hiveDefensePerSize` 400 a size: the strata ARE the ground defence before multipliers (`SwarmNexus.apply` tops vanilla's base up to `ThreatStrata.standing` = strata less 400 per stratum a front holds; `IncursionManager.nexusAnchoredDefense` the same). Fed above break-even they thicken 400 per `colonyGrowthBaseDays` x size at full pace, starving they thin 400 per `starveDaysPerSize` (`ThreatColonyManager.advanceFedGrowth`); growth still held by a front or saturation, hunger never. Saturation takes 400 per `strataSuppressDaysPerSize` (new knob, 300) disruption days it adds (`ThreatGroundFronts.saturationSlice`: a day down 30 = 40 strata; a 94 FP squadron over Beta Prisma I adds ~3 days a day = ~4 strata a day, ~95 days a stratum; 1,400 FP ~25 a day, ~16 days); tactical bombardment still wears structures only. The size is the strata over 400 rounded down, 1 at least, capped at the hive's max (`syncSize`: `growColony` up, `ThreatRazing.reduceSize` down, "Stratum Razed" / "Hive Starving" notices; a front holding every stratum left wins at once); at 0 the hive is gone (`collapse`: the front's ground victory, else "Hive Razed" / "Hive Starved" and `eradicate`). SHRINK_MARGIN hysteresis gone; the vitality tooltip shows "Strata: 1,150, size 3 at 1,200" and the Growth line's days come from the strata (`daysToNextSize`, `daysToLoseSize`). REVERSES fact 194 (razing never desizes) and the 22:36 entry (1); the 22:45 "Keep 400" stands as the size step. The old progress clock (`ThreatIncData.growthProgressDays`) serves only the vitality path with size upkeep off. Judgement calls: chunk size per disruption day (300 = `defenseWearDays`, so a bombardment that puts the structures fully down has taken one stratum); the colonies table / Bombard prompt do not yet quote strata a day. Jar 23:16, untested. -> `hive-stocks-and-upkeep.md` "THE STRATA", `suppression-balance.md` principle 5, `code-map.md` ThreatStrata
- **DECISION 2026-10-24 23:42 (the user: "assuming the strata is the troops for threat I'm expecting a siege's attrition to permanently lower strata for every troop lost even if siege against threat fails right. And if threat reinforce their own planet that is directly increasing strata"; then "Strata during siege could drop due to attrition and not being fed due to blockade in space etc").** THE STRATA ARE THE TROOPS. (1) A landing on a hive bleeds the strata standing by THE ATTRITION RULE (`ThreatGroundFronts.bleedHiveStrata`, `worldLossPerDay` on `ThreatStrata.standing`), every tick, for good - the size steps down with them ("Stratum Bled" under the besieger's crest), a front holding every stratum left wins, 0 is the end; a failed siege still leaves the hive thinner. Until now only the troops broken up over the hive bled and the strata were a wall whole strata were taken from. (2) Swarm ships broken up over their own hive (`breakUpForHive`) land as strata (`ThreatStrata.add`); the separate hive-troop count (`$threatinc_hiveTroops`) is gone - `ThreatStrata.of` folds an older save's into the strata. `Theatre.HIVE.defenderStrength` is vanilla's figure alone (the Nexus top-up carries the strata); the board's world line reads "N from M strata standing". (3) Hunger was never paused by a front or saturation, and a human orbit sets the blockade cut (`ThreatBlockade.hiveCut` -> `importCut`), so a besieged hive thins from attrition and starvation at once; growth is held meanwhile. `ThreatStrata.add` reapplies the industries once the figure has moved a point (`reapplyIfMoved`) so the defence is live. Jar 23:42, untested. -> `ground-war-orbit-control.md` break-up paragraph, `hive-stocks-and-upkeep.md` THE STRATA
- **DECISION 2026-10-25 (the user: "when threat siege where do their troops come from? ... they should probably break up their ships so it's actually a tracked resource that's consumed. On their planets troops are just the strata"; chose "ships only" with the planner buying the landing).** SHIPS ARE THE SOLDIERS. Until now a strike carried a FREE pool - `strikeTroopsPerPoint` 20 x difficulty points, fixed at launch, charged to nothing (not the ledger, not strata, not hulls; about 0.8 troops a FP of the strike against 5 a FP broken up, so a sixth of the strike's hulls turned to soldiers for nothing) - and broke hulls up only for a beachhead shortfall (`beachheadLanding`) or a guard's top-up (`fabricateTroops`). Now (1) the pool is 0 (settings, LunaSettings, migration v18; a value restores it on top), (2) every landing and every reinforcement pass breaks hulls up at `fabricateTroopsPerFP` through `ThreatStrikeFGI.embark` (fabrication bank + flag, so the log and the census read it as a break-up; abstract strikes via `fabricatedFP`), a first landing max(300, `beachheadTroops`), a reinforcement `fabricateNeed`; (3) `landable` = fragments aboard + `hullTroops` replaces `troopsAboard` in every gate (`performRaid`, the daily siege's nothing-to-land, `guardShare`) and `abstractLanding` replaces max(share, aboard) in the abstract siege's land-early tests; (4) the staged muster buys the landing: `IncursionManager.landingPoints(target)` = max(300, beachhead) / 5 / 25 FP a point, and `stagedPlan` sizes the orbit on the points LEFT after it (both stop tests), so a prong priced by the offensive carries it too. The legacy garrison muster and the strike gate (`pickStrikeTarget`, the orbit fight with the whole fleet) are unchanged. Strata stay the hive's own body - at 400 a stratum an away landing would gut the sender. Jar 00:13, untested. -> `ground-war-sieges.md` "Sizing the landing", `strikes-staged.md` Size, `swarm-strategy.md` What a strike does

- **PROPOSAL 2026-10-25 (the user: "i would like the strata to be the threat ... when a colony absorbs a fleet it is absorbed into and increase the strata ... if a colony needs a space fleet it generates one and decrements its own strata ... staggered ... same animation ... disruption and heavy batteries and all military builds can completely go. the fabrication core remains as the sole building that creates strata from nothing. and the fabrication core needs metals and fuel"). NOTHING BUILT.** Reviewed and specced in `strata-are-the-threat.md`: the fabrication bank is already a hidden per-colony strata pool (bud = `maintainColonyGarrisons`, absorb = `fitNavyToSpare` / `breakUpForHive`, home = `settleLedger`), so the design merges the bank into `ThreatStrata`, makes the Core the sole producer (metals + fuel -> strata a month, vanilla deficit), moves strata a ship at a time at `strataFlowPerDay` with the break-up animation both ways, decays fleets at 0.04 a month in place of supplies, and deletes the Nexus / Bastion / Command / Ground Defenses / Heavy Batteries, disruption, guns and raids from the hive theatre. Four decisions put to the user (the size table - linear 400 / 1,000 / doubling from 400, recommended doubling; the hive off supplies entirely; return fire from the strata; build order A bank->strata, B off supplies, C staggered flow, D military layer gone). Section 5 holds the arithmetic: one figure means growth, navy and the landing price are one rate.
- **DECISION 2026-10-25 (the user, round 1 on `strata-are-the-threat.md`; NOTHING BUILT - "still designing"):** (1) the hive size table DOUBLES from 400 (400, 800, 1,600 ... 51,200 at size 8; a seed stays 800, a fortress is bled, bombed or beaten in orbit, not landed on); (2) a bombarded hive fires NOTHING back - "they can convert strata into a fleet to fight back" (the posture buds against bombers; raids on hives gone); (3) build order A bank->strata + Core, B off supplies, C staggered flow, D military layer gone - but no build starts while the design is open; (4) the anti-snowball is NOT upkeep: the Core demands metals and fuel scaled to the world it feeds ("a size 8 needs an import source of 8 of each"), vanilla accessibility caps what a world imports, "if needs met exactly it doesn't grow it needs surplus to grow", a shortage thins strata until the size its imports feed. A strict "surplus above demand" rule deadlocks (the biggest refinery world could never grow), so met-in-full = grow, one unit short = hold, two short = starve.
- **DECISION 2026-10-25 (the user, round 2 on `strata-are-the-threat.md`; NOTHING BUILT):** (1) **fleets do not hang in orbit** - "they are fabricated reactively when the world is attacked. Once fabricated they have no upkeep": no standing garrisons, no patrol-table floor, no decay; a Defend fleet buds on attack and is reabsorbed after, strikes / shifts / seeds / scouts are the other reasons a fleet exists; (2) the hive is OFF SUPPLIES entirely (forges, the supplies stock, size upkeep, feeding swarms, the hive cover, Threat fleet holds all go; metals + fuel into the Core are the only inputs); (3) at the cap, fed in surplus, NOTHING happens - the hive's mass is bounded by its worlds' caps and grows only by seeding; (4) hive accessibility is NOT distance to a producer but **aggregate strata density, like gravity** ("a pull to the location with the highest concentration of strata which has highest accessibility ... the threat could convert its backline strata to ships and reabsorb them at the worlds by the front ... front is strong but perhaps all the producers at the back have now low size and accessibility so stop outputting ... super dynamic") - spec s3: density = sum of S_j / (1 + (d/10 ly)^2), 10% accessibility a doubling over 6,400, clamp -30 / +50, self-weight open; SHIFT (bud at the back, fly, absorb at the front) is the hive's lever. The user asked whether vanilla's Core-distance accessibility penalty can be REMOVED rather than refunded: no - javap on the API jar finds `accessibilityDistFromCOM` read in no API class (PopulationAndInfrastructure applies only "No spaceport" and "Colony size"); it is applied by the obfuscated core and the setting is global, so the hive's accessibility is taken over wholesale by one "Strata density" line landing the total (as `applyPortDisruption` lands a target). Round 3 questions in s11: density self-weight, how humans size a siege against buds, flat vs proportional flow, shifting with no producer floor.
- **DECISION 2026-10-25 (the user, round 3 on `strata-are-the-threat.md`; NOTHING BUILT):** (1) density **self-weight 0** - "In system imports have no accessibility modifier right. So seeded system has everything it needs to grow as we pick systems with volatiles and ores. As soon as a second system is seeded that's when accessibility matters as it will be importing from the initial system." CHECKED: vanilla has NO in-system exemption (every in-faction import is capped by the IMPORTER's shipping, 10 x accessibility + 5 a commodity; only a world's OWN industry output reaches it free), so the density makes the premise true instead - same-system siblings count in full, a pair or cluster bootstraps to 8 on its own plants, but a LONE importer in an empty system holds at size 3 (round-4 question: accept / seed new systems in pairs / clamp floor -20%); (2) NPC sieges sized against the buds over the planned stay: min(strata, flow x days) / 5 x margin, and the council shortens the stay to what it can out-bud (a fortress is 205 FP a day of stay); (3) flow 2% of the strata a day, floor 400 (size <= 6: 80 FP a day; 7: 102; 8: 205; a seed is budded out in two days); (4) NO producer floor on shifting - the density feedback is the brake; with self-weight 0 a shift lifts the receiver's siblings, so the pass shifts into systems.

- **BUILT 2026-10-25 (phase C of `strata-are-the-threat.md`, jar 14:16, UNTESTED, local commit): the garrisons go; the strata bud, shift and are absorbed.** `ThreatBudding` (new): a hive world keeps nothing over it; on a trigger - any hostile weight within ORBIT_HOLD_RANGE with less standing against it (`poll` -> `ThreatPosture.rally`, whose gather counts `buddableFP(system)` and whose tail calls `defend`) - it buds a Defend fleet: an order in market memory (`KEY_KIND` defend / shift, `KEY_WANT`, `KEY_POOL`), filled at the flow each poll (`flowPerDay` = max(`strataFlowPerDay` 400, `strataFlowFractionPerDay` 0.02 x strata); `take`: staged points, then strata to a stratum for its own defence / `shiftFloorStrata` for a shift), the rising glows (`ThreatFabricationVisual.play(..., rising)`), spawned WHOLE when full (`ThreatFleetComposer.createSized`, seated in `garrisonsFor`, `placeGarrisonSwarm`), drained back if the attacker leaves, spawned short when doomed (`doomed`: ground / yesterday's enemy loss rate < remaining / flow; the rate from `ThreatStrata.add` -> `noteLoss`), a sibling's share ordered as a shift to the world. Absorbing (`absorbLingering` / `absorbHulls`): a fleet over its own world with the orbit quiet `defendLingerDays` (10; a shift on arrival at once, `ABSORB_ON_ARRIVAL`) loses hulls smallest-first at the flow (`applyFleetLosses`) into the strata to the cap; at the cap it stands. Shift (`shift`, from `redistributeGarrisons` in place of the deleted `redistributeByPressure`): one order a poll - an attacked world short of an answer first, else PRESS -> the hive system nearest a known human world, CONSOLIDATE -> the system with the most strata (the world with the most room), EXPAND nothing; the donor the nearest quiet world of another system above the floor; sent by `sendReinforcement`. Strikes: `launchStrike`'s staged branch holds until the bill is in the colony's staged points (`strikeStaged`; `orderStrike` refreshed each ask, lapses 60 d) and the poll bites `spendStrikeFund` / `poolHiveBanks` at the staging system's flow (`advanceStrike`); the plan's budget counts the staged points. The posture's standing figures read 0 (`floorFP`, `minimumFP`, `baseCount`, `rowsFP`, `oneSwarmFP`, `baseReserve`): `wantFP` is the pressure's share and the line; `maintainColonyGarrisons` only prunes; `splitLaunchSwarm` deleted; `fillGarrisonNow` 0. Board: a **Flow** column (`Entry.flow` = `ThreatBudding.flowText`). Phase-B leftovers fixed: `IncursionManager.getPhase`, the posture's forge count and `ThreatMissionIntel.isStrikePlatform` gated strikes on a forge no hive has - now `ThreatColonyManager.coreStands`. Deliberate: scouts / patrols / raiders still draw strata at once; a fleet home settles at once on despawn (`settleLedger`); old garrisons and the Abyss bootstrap wave migrate by the linger rule; the Nexus and `ThreatHulls`' patrol read stand until phase D. Knobs added `strataFlowPerDay`, `strataFlowFractionPerDay`, `defendLingerDays`. Verify: nothing over a quiet hive after 10 d; a bomber over a hive -> rising glows, "Budding: X buds N FP", a whole Defend fleet; "stands down" when it leaves; "a strike of N FP buds at" then the launch; an old save logs "absorbs N FP of"; a PRESS shift order.
- **BUILT 2026-10-25 (phase D of `strata-are-the-threat.md`, jar 14:47, UNTESTED, local commit): the military layer goes, bombardment is a strata exchange, one Attack order.** `ThreatColonyManager.retireMilitaryLayer` strips the Nexus, batteries, Bastion and Command off every hive each essentials sweep (classes kept for saves, inert); `FabricationCore` is unraidable and tops the ground defence up to `ThreatStrata.standing`; RAID is off on a hive. The HIVE theatre has no structures, no return fire, no drop loss. `ThreatGroundFronts.hiveRaze` is the one path: `bombardStrataPerFPDay` (1) x fp strata a day for `bombardFuelPerStratum` (2) fuel each, through `ThreatStrata.add`; `siegeSlice` carries a razer id, `bombardFuelPerDay(market, fp)` / `bombardDaysFor(market, fuel, fp)` are the market-aware overloads every caller uses; a dry purge with no marines blockades up to `siegeOrbitDays`. Player dialog: `ThreatincMarketCMD.strataBombardMenu` / `strataBombardPrompt`. Humans size against buds: `ThreatIntel.Report.budFP` / `budDays` (the bud is the swarm in `nearFP` and each world). Attack: `IncursionManager.attackPlan` / `attackBlockFacts` / `launchAttack` -> `launchSiegeExpedition(..., fuelGiven)` attack mode; board: one Attack button a hive row, Fleet / Fuel / Marines ladders (`ThreatFactionView.addAttackLadders`, tiers on `ThreatIncursionIntel`), `addAttackPrompt`. 12 knobs retired (Luna migration 21), `attackMedShare` 0.5 added. Judgement calls and the verify list are in the spec's section 10 D block.
- **BUILT 2026-10-25 (phase E of `strata-are-the-threat.md`, jar 14:55, UNTESTED, local commit): the swarm's landing consumes a human world.** `ThreatGroundWarCondition.apply` cuts every industry's output by the seized share (`ThreatGroundFronts.infestShare` = districts held / size); the front grows `infestGrowthPerDay` = `coreStrataPerSizeMonth` x size x share / 30 into its strata (`feedOnWorld`, in `tickFront` after the attrition); the fronts table's Held cell reads "+N/d". A ground victory seeds the hive at the army's strata (`convertConquered(planet, market, armyStrata)`) and flags `CONSUME_TO_FLAG` = the world's size: while `ThreatColonyManager.consumingTo` > 0 the Core demands nothing (`coreDemand(market, c)`) and the hive grows a size per `starveDaysPerSize` days (`coreState.consuming`), unflagged at the size. No new knobs. ALL FIVE PHASES ARE NOW BUILT AND UNTESTED; the spec's section 10 holds each phase's verify list.
- **FIXED 2026-10-25 (jar 18:05, untested, local commit): the strike-fund flood.** The user's first test of phases A-E: bombarding Beta Prisma I (780 strata, size 2) and it read size 8 with 70k+ strata. Not the bombardment: `ThreatColonyManager.migrateBankToStrata` (once a save, the first poll after load) poured the retired hive-wide strike fund (46,689 FP = 233k strata in that save) into the worlds with the most ROOM first, and a size-2 world has 76,000 strata of room to its cap. Now every world takes at most one size's worth above where it stands (`ThreatStrata.span`): its own bank first, then its strata-weighted share of the fund; the rest of the retired figures is lost and logged ("a size's worth a world at most; N FP lost"). Same fix: a budded fleet spawns AT the planet (`placeGarrisonSwarm`, as vanilla's patrols) so it comes out of the world; and the hive's human demands (food, goods, drugs: vanilla Population & Infrastructure) are cancelled on every recompute by `FabricationCore.apply` (`getDemandReductionFromOther` flat 100 at any size) - the essentials sweep's size-sized flat lagged a size change, which is why the size-8 Beta Prisma showed food shortages. Also learned: debug logging is a LunaLib value (`threatinc_debugLogging` in settings.json is shadowed by Luna's csv default of false) and the LunaLib settings panel crashed on the user's machine with `MissingFormatArgumentException '% A'` in `LunaSettingsUISettingsPanel.recreatePanel` - no "%" exists in this mod's csv or mod_info, so it is another mod's text; the user's toggle never saved, hence no log.
- **FIXED 2026-10-25 (jar 18:30, untested, local commit): the hive colony screen after the user's second test round.** (1) No "No spaceport -100%" line: a hive's `hasSpaceport` flag is set (`ensureFabricationCore` sweep, `ensureSpaceport` after the retirement, `HiveVitalityCondition.apply`) so PopulationAndInfrastructure never adds it, and `ThreatColonyManager.applyHiveAccessibility(market)` (now per world, also run from the condition's apply each recompute) lifts `NO_PORT_MOD_ID` = `ind_population_0` before landing the density total - the user: "if we cant get rid of that might as well give them all spaceports". (2) No "No active comm relay" / "Domestic goods demand met" stability lines: `HUMAN_STABILITY_MODS` (`ind_population_0..2`, `CommRelayCondition.COMM_RELAY_MOD_ID`) unmodified in the condition's apply. (3) No human demand on ANY hive industry (Surgat's Mining short of drugs): the condition's apply multiplies every non-Core industry's demand for food / drugs / domestic / luxury goods by 0 (vanilla's `ResourceDepositsCondition` pattern, conditions apply after industries) - if a Mining tooltip still lists drugs, this pattern does not reach demand and the fallback is `getDemandReductionFromOther` on Mining (cancels its heavy machinery too, Core-supplied locally, harmless). (4) The colony screen's **Growth figure reads the strata**: vanilla's figure is incoming weight / the size's weight span (300 x 2^(size - 3); Surgat's -1.88% = hazard -50 + accessibility +5 over 2,400, checked) and never resizes an NPC market (`increaseMarketSize` is player-only), so `ThreatHiveGrowth` (a transient `MarketImmigrationModifier`, re-added each sweep) drops vanilla's lines and lands the vitality tooltip's own figure as a share of the next size (`perDay`: starving negative, held 0, at the cap 0) and pins the population weight to the strata's progress through the size; a size-6 world fed in full reads +9.4% a month. (5) Wording (the user: "keep strata for the layers/sizing but replace instances where swarm would fit"): "Swarm density" (accessibility line, tooltips, csv, spec), the defence flat "Swarm" (was "Fabrication strata"), notices "Swarm rising / settling / absorbed", "Swarm on the move", "The swarm spreads through the ruins of", "is being consumed by the swarm", "The swarm is taking root"; "Strata: N, size S at M", "Strata held", "stratum" (districts) and the razing lines stay. Judgement call: the condition-time landing of the density total may run before the core adds "Proximity to other colonies" (+20%), in which case the total reads 20% high until the next poll re-lands it.

A HIVE INDUSTRY PRODUCES AT ITS SIZE.** The Core's demands stay (metals at size, fuel at size - 2); the supply side closes vanilla's three gaps: `threatinc_hiveOutputOffset` 2 is added to a hive Mining's and Fuel Production's `supplyBonusFromOther` (rare ore and volatiles at size + deposit, fuel at size; ore rides along at size + 2) and to Refining's `demandReductionFromOther` (ore at size, not size + 2), re-asserted by `FabricationCore.apply` -> `ThreatColonyManager.applyHiveOutput` through vanilla's own bonus stats, which each industry reads inside its apply() whatever the order. - **A siege arrives, idles over the hive and goes home without landing (the user, 2026-10-26 evening, Sindria just conquered, a Hegemony siege "idled around the planet then did no ground actions and left"). FIXED (built, untested).** No log of it: `threatinc_debugLogging` was false in `saves/common/LunaSettings/threatinc.json.data` (Debug mode is a different toggle; the game log held no `[ThreatInc]` line since 2026-10-24). Read from the code: a hive's defence AND its counter-attack are the strata standing (`ThreatGroundFronts.HIVE`), the NPC landing is sized at planning to `beachheadNeeded` = strata / 0.75 x 1.25 of TODAY's strata (`IncursionManager.raidStrNeededAt`, `nexusAnchoredDefense`), and the strata grow while the siege musters and sails (a fed hive 200 x size a month, a conquered world consuming to its size a span per 90 days - Sindria's hive, size 2 at 800, +8.9 a day): on arrival `beachheadSurvives` fails by the growth, `siegeNoDoomedLanding` (true in the user's settings) lands nothing, the siege carries no ordnance (`hivePlan` said the troops landed on day 0) so `orbitDoneHere` is true and every pass returns, and the fleets hold the orbit `siegeStayDays` then go home - no notice. Two fixes: (1) the planner sizes on the strata at ARRIVAL - `ThreatStrata.atArrival` = standing + `coreState.strataPerDay` x `threatinc_siegeGrowthDays` (new knob, 90 = muster 14 + passage), clipped to the cap, growth only; `nexusAnchoredDefense` reads it, so the board's Siege tiers quote it too; (2) a live NPC siege whose pass finds the landing doomed with nothing left to bombard with calls off at once with a notice quoting marines aboard against the landing needed (`ThreatPurgeFGI.callOffShort`; the off-screen daily siege already ended the world on "ready"). `strata-are-the-threat.md` 6 "Landing".
- **DECISION 2026-10-26 evening (the user, on the Sindria screenshot - the Diktat with Volturn under a Threat army and Sindria a hive, its hammers sailing for Hadreel, Zeta Ranau and Damar's Star: "remember they just attack now its not a siege its not a bombing run its not a hunt its an attack, and the attack takes whatever it can use ... fuel destroys strata, ships destroy fleet ships, marines can land and fight the swarm. an expedition should take whatever it can get. also why is sindria not striking a threat world in its own system??? ... where is the distance based prioritisation"). BUILT (javac clean, jar waits on the game), untested.** Three causes read from the code: (1) `ThreatPlays.invade` ordered every in-reach hive EASIEST first (`IncursionManager.easiestFirst`), no distance term; (2) `homeUnderAttack` (2026-10-20) returned the council before any play while Volturn stood invaded - the hive in its own system could not be attacked; (3) `payingBase` / `siegeCanPay` / the launch refused a landing short of marines ("no paying base in 3: {marines=3}" every pass in the 10-24 log). Built: knob `threatinc_invadeNearestFirst` (true) - `ThreatPlays.nearestFirst`: worlds not on cooldown first, then by ly from the cluster's nearest base (0 in an own system), easiest among equals; THE HOME FIGHT - `ThreatGroundFronts.homeFightIn(faction, system)`: with home under attack the council runs `invade(..., homeOnly)` over the invaded systems only, the planner's two siege gates and the plays' stand-down / hold let a play at that system through; knob `threatinc_npcAttackTakesWhatItHas` (true) - the council gate and the launch no longer refuse for marines or arms (the pooled ones sail, the short-landing pricing buys ordnance to raze what they cannot cover, `shortLanding` = max(1, have) so 1 marine prices the whole raze, the flotilla is not trimmed to the marines, the fuel gate prices the passage alone and the ordnance is drawn as far as the depot holds it). Also: the "Verbose Logging" toggle is the log (`threatinc_debugLogging`), and it read False in the user's own settings screenshot - "Debug Mode" is the playtest mode. `strata-are-the-threat.md` 6.
- **DECISION 2026-10-27 12:40 (the user, on hw288c - no grand fleet sailed in 1,300 days, every base "fuel 0 of 180,000-355,000" on a fuel income of 0: "i thought the point was every attack goes with whatever it has, they can still land marines no and defend the orbit? why is fuel stopping them?"): OPTION 1 - A GRAND FLEET SAILS ON HULLS, THE PASSAGE AND THE CRUISE'S SUPPLIES; FUEL BEYOND THE PASSAGE IS TAKEN AS FOUND, NO KILL BANK, A HOP IS DECIDED FROM WHAT IS ABOARD, AND A DRY STAY LANDS ITS MARINES AND HOLDS THE ORBIT OVER ITS FRONT.** Built 12:50 (`ThreatGrandFleets.muster` / `launch` / `hopNeed`, `ThreatPurgeFGI`'s stay; `grandFleetKills` and `grandFleetOrdnanceMargin` unused), `docs/grand-fleet.md` 12; round 59 is its test. Option 2 (keep the bank, ship fuel to the bases) was not taken.
- **Every NPC siege sails with 0 marines (2026-10-26 run, the first on the strata jar: "siege of 1450 FP sails from Raesvelg for Sindria, 0 marines aboard" with 16,872 held; the Diktat's, the League's, the independents' all 0; the 10-24/25 logs had 1,224 / 2,471 / 809). ROOT CAUSE, FIXED (built, untested).** `IncursionManager.needAndWear` sizes the landing at the defence left after the flotilla's bombardment plan (`bombardPlan(target, fp)[1]`, the defence then as a fraction of now). With strata razed for good (ThreatStrata, 2026-10-25) a few fleet points' plan wears a hive to NOTHING over siegeOrbitDays, so the worn need is 0: the planner's "Saturate or siege ... landing 0 marines of 16,872 held" and the launch's wants[0] = 0. Then over the hive `siegePass` says "nothing left to land, no siege" and the doomed branch returns - the Hegemony's idle over Sindria both times. Fix under `npcAttackTakesWhatItHas`: an NPC landing is sized at the UNWORN need (`raidStrNeededAt(targets, 0)`, the strata at arrival) - the marines sail and land once the bombardment has done its part; the player's tiers keep the worn figure. With it, `razeWorlds` no longer saturates a hive in place of the marines it lacks ("sieges - the attack takes the N marines it has of the M the landing wants and razes the rest"), and `siegeCanPay` gates on the passage alone as the launch does. Two log lines grew: the siege's launch line now carries ordnance and razing fuel; the invade pass line names each refused system with siegeCanPay's `lastRefusal` ("cannot pay (pays 9 of 38 points at Cruor (... hulls free 229 FP))") instead of a count. WHY THE DIKTAT STRUCK LIGHT YEARS AWAY in that run: `homeUnderAttack` is OFF in the user's settings.json (2026-10-20), so the home fight never applied; Cruor had no Waystation until that run (`isBase` = military + depot) and Volturn is the invaded world, so the Diktat's nearest base to Askonia was Mazalot (the League's) and every system read "cannot pay" - its hulls: 229 free of 864, three plays out at Mazalot. Cruor built its Waystation in-run; the next pass can sail from it at 0 ly. The Hegemony's 8 fleets "running in circles" over Sindria: the orbit was contested (965 FP of Defense Swarms reported, hostiles 2090) and a siege hunts the swarms before any pass (`siegeFightsForOrbit`); no pass line for Sindria in that log.
- **A fleet running from another holds no orbit (the user, 2026-10-26: "any fleet that is fleeing another fleet should not count as contesting the orbit" - Threat fleets fly in and away constantly). BUILT jar 20:58, untested.** `ThreatGroundFronts.countsNear` (the one filter behind `pointsNear`, `hostileFleetsNear`, so the orbit contest and the daily siege's strikes) drops a fleet `ThreatGroundFronts.fleeing` says is running: vanilla's `CampaignFleetAIAPI.isFleeing()` or the `FleetAIFlags.NEAREST_FLEEING_FROM` memory key. Honest caveat: the "contested" reading of the Hegemony's eight circling fleets over Sindria was inferred from `SiegeRaidAction.canRaid` / `directFleets` (orbitHeld -> no pass, hunt), not from the log, which had no pass line for Sindria at all.
- **NPC attack strategy to be rethought (the user, 2026-10-26 evening): the grand fleet - PROPOSAL written, nothing built.** Sizing an attack against a fluid hive (strata, sizes) keeps failing; instead each faction saves up for one grand fleet sized to its military score, which sails when mustered and destroys the nearest Threat world, then the next, until fuel or supplies send it home. `grand-fleet.md` holds the doctrine, knobs and the three choices waiting on the user (replace the council's sieges or run alongside; score = standing navy FP vs council weight vs tier table; stay until strata 0 or move on once the front holds).
- **THE GRAND FLEET - BUILT 2026-10-26 evening (jar 21:18), untested; the user's three choices: replace the council's sieges, score = the board's Navy figure, stay until strata 0.** `ThreatGrandFleets.daily` (from `ThreatWarCouncil.advance`): per NPC faction at war, one grand fleet (`grandFleetsPerFaction`); size = clamp(`grandFleetShare` 0.5 x navy, `grandFleetMinFP` 300, `grandFleetMaxFP` 3000), never above the navy; target = the nearest known Threat world a base of the faction (or an ally's staging host) reaches; waits for free hulls >= size (noteHullShort) and the base's pooled fuel >= passage + `grandFleetCruiseDays` (120) x bombardFuelPerDay and supplies >= siegeSuppliesPerPoint(cruise) x points (noteShort + `ThreatConvoys.stageForPlay(base, "grand:<fid>", ...)` so convoys fill it); then `launch`: a `ThreatPurgeFGI` over every hive in the system, raid duration = the cruise, EVERY marine and armament pooled drawn, fuel = passage + ordnance, supplies = deposit + hold, notice "Grand Fleet Sails". Leg done (`ThreatPurgeFGI.notifyActionFinished` -> `legDone`, or `callOffShort` -> `legDone(forced)`): the next nearest known Threat system (not this one) whose passage + passage home + `grandFleetReserveDays` (20) of burn the ordnance and hold still cover -> `hop`: a new leg from the system just fought in (`ThreatPurgeFGI.hopFrom`, read in `initActions`: the travel action starts there, no departure wait), marines/arms/ordnance (less the passage)/hold handed over, live fleets adopted (`adoptFleetsFrom`), the old leg `markHopped` + `endImmediately` (no refund, no settlement); none -> home (forced: abort at once). A grand-fleet leg bombards with or without marines (`siegePass`). `ThreatPlays.plan` under the knob runs `reconForGrandFleet` only (nearest stale/unknown cluster); `IncursionManager.launchPlanned` returns null for NPCs. Faction tab: one line, `ThreatGrandFleets.boardLine`. Knob `npcGrandFleet` false restores the council. `grand-fleet.md`.
- **RUN gf1 (2026-10-26 night, the first grand fleet test, the user's save cloned, 364 days):** two faults, both fixed in the 22:03 jar (`docs/grand-fleet.md` 6). (1) No grand fleet but the Diktat's sailed: the fuel bank was `grandFleetCruiseDays` of razing at the fleet's full rate (723,000 fuel for the Hegemony's 3,000 FP) - now the bank is the kill, the targets' strata on arrival x the fuel a stratum x `grandFleetOrdnanceMargin` (`ThreatGrandFleets.razeFuel`), and a hop needs the next kill's fuel plus both passages. (2) The Diktat (navy 243, under the 300 floor) sailed its whole navy four times into Sindria's 1,214-4,382 FP of swarms and was called off each time: the floor is now a floor (`musterFP`), the target is the nearest world the fleet outweighs by its faction's report (`swarmsSeen` = `IncursionManager.systemSwarms` x `grandFleetOutweigh`), and a called-off system waits `grandFleetRetryDays` (`calledOff`, `onCooldown`; the notice is "Grand Fleet Turns Home"). The hop is still untested.
- **Why did the Diktat's grand fleet come home with "0 ground actions" and no siege line (gf1)?** `ThreatPurgeFGI.callOff` - the vanilla orbit weighing on arrival (1,214 FP of swarms against 366) - logs "Siege called off over X" AFTER `abort()`, so the ended/returns lines come first in the log; read the two lines after "home after N legs".

- **RUN gf2 (2026-10-26 night, the second grand fleet test, the day-4036 StarLord save cloned, ~320 days, 0 exceptions):** every faction sails and the outweigh rule works; three faults fixed in the 22:52 jar (`docs/grand-fleet.md` 7). (1) A leg landed all its marines and turned home two days later, the world credited 0 and its razing fuel unburnt: a grand-fleet leg now STAYS over a hive until its strata are gone (`ThreatPurgeFGI.dailyDay` `stays`, `landStaying`, `stayWindow` - the landing keeps the world, the days after bombard, orbit done it holds over its front; dry ends it). (2) All four hops aborted silently on arrival: the hop's route was built from the stock actions (below) - rebuilt in `initActions`. (3) The Path's leg mauled in transit came home at 25% with nothing said: the end notice/log carry the strength left, under vanilla's abort line it reads "Grand Fleet Lost"; every abort logs "Expedition abort state". DECIDED 2026-10-27 (the user): the bank is `grandFleetKills` (2) kills - the target, then the next nearest it outweighs from there, the passages between and the one home (`ThreatGrandFleets.chainFrom`). The player faction never sails (`ThreatGrandFleets.daily` skips `isPlayerFaction`); the 29 sail notices the user saw were NPC fleets re-sailing after two-day legs.
- **Why did every grand-fleet hop abort with "0 ground actions" and strength 100% (gf2)?** `GenericRaidFGI.initActions` ends by building the route from its `actions` (wait at the source, travel from it, raid, return). `ThreatPurgeFGI.initActions` swapped the travel action and removed the wait AFTER that, so the route's segments pointed at actions the leg no longer had; vanilla's `FleetGroupIntel.spawnFleet` (the player in range) removes every action up to the current segment's and `abort()`s when none is left - no log line. Fixed: the hop's route is rebuilt from its own actions (`removeRoute` + `createRoute`), the old leg's route removed on the hop (`ThreatGrandFleets.hop`). Rule: anything that changes `actions` after `super.initActions()` must rebuild the route.

- **RUN gf3 (2026-10-27 morning, the third grand fleet test, the day-4036 StarLord save cloned, 244 days, 0 exceptions):** the stay works off-screen (bombardment razes strata: a 3,000-FP fleet razes 3,000 strata a day, 13 "Hive Razed" + 10 "Hive Eradicated") but every kill ended the leg as an abort credited 0: `IncursionManager.abortPurgesAgainst` (the purge stand-down when every target is dead) fired before `legDone` - FIXED, it skips grand fleets (06:45 jar). Live legs (the player in Askonia) landed everything and `stayOnDefend` handed all 12 fleets to Defend, leaving `legDone` 0 FP - FIXED: the live stay in `ThreatPurgeFGI.siegePass` (`liveSlice` a pass, unspent, while ordnance lasts and the hive stands), `stayOnDefend` returns for a grand fleet. The two-kill bank sails near pairs and puts far pairs at 150,000-515,000 fuel; `legDone` hops to the banked chain's next world first (`GrandFleet.chainIds`, the user 2026-10-27), the nearest affordable after it (06:55 jar). Harness: never `tail -F` the `run-<tag>.txt` the driver appends to (Windows lock). -> `docs/grand-fleet.md` 8
- **Why did every grand-fleet leg come home "0 worlds destroyed" after razing its world (gf3)?** `IncursionManager.abortPurgesAgainst` - the stand-down for a purge whose whole target list is dead - aborted the leg the moment its last hive died ("Purge Stands Down ... nothing to burn", abort state "SiegeRaidAction, daily siege on"), before the daily siege's "gone" could end the action and `ThreatGrandFleets.legDone` count the world and hop. Fixed 2026-10-27: the stand-down skips grand fleets.

Fixed 2026-10-27 (07:37 jar): a grand fleet's leg sails on whenever its action ends unaborted; lost means strength below `getGroupAbortsMissionFPFraction`.

- **Is a hive's accessibility limited by isolation (the user's game, Sindria 2026-10-27, tooltip "-15% Isolation from other colonies, +48% Swarm density" = 33%)?** Not by vanilla's line: `applyHiveAccessibility` lands the density as the WHOLE total, the "Swarm density" line being the difference to whatever vanilla added, so the -15% changed nothing. Lifted 12:57 jar (untested): `core_base` (vanilla's isolation / proximity mod, `CommodityMarketData.computeBaseAccessibility`, re-applied `modifyFlatAlways` each economy tick) is unmodified with the No-spaceport line, so the tooltip reads "Swarm density: +33%" alone. **But the figure itself is the finding:** Sindria sits 27-40 ly from every other hive (Askonia west, the swarm east: Shero 26.7 ly, Shevar 35-37.5, Ranau 36, Damar's Star 37, Khore 39-40) and still reads +26..36%, 7-8 units, enough to feed size 8 - the save's 811,000 strata over 37 worlds at a tenth weight is 75,000, twelve times `densityRef`. The "Density, worked" table was checked against ONE 51,200 fortress; a mature swarm sums past the falloff and distance stops limiting size sector-wide. The user's intent is "isolated colonies cant grow large". **DECIDED 2026-10-27 (the user: "Range 20 ly"), BUILT jar 13:09 untested (new key, no Luna migration):** only hive worlds within `densityRangeLY` 20 count toward a world's density (`ThreatColonyManager.strataDensity`; knob 0 = every world, the old rule), the curve unchanged inside it - Sindria with no hive in range holds at size 3 (density 0, -30%, 2 units); same-system siblings weigh 1 whatever the range, so the pair bootstrap and the hw277-hw282 stalls (supply-side, every held world at +50%) are untouched. -> `strata-are-the-threat.md` "Density, worked", `hive-economy.md` "Shipping capacity"
- **Why did a grand fleet's fleets sit over a dead world after razing it (the user's game, Sindria, 2026-10-27)?** Live, vanilla's raid stage ends by passes spent or by its duration (the cruise), and `SiegeRaidAction.performRaid` never counts a pass against a world no longer in the economy, so the dead world held the stage and `legDone` never ran. FIXED 09:32 jar (untested): `passDeadWorlds` spends a gone or no-longer-hive target's passes every frame. -> `docs/grand-fleet.md` 11

- **Can the Threat occupy a human world without owning it (the user, 2026-10-27)?** Yes: ownership moves only on our `setFactionId`; phase E already infests and seizes industries one by one; `$core_noDeciv` stops vanilla decivilising a 0-stability world; a disrupted industry makes nothing (grow on its base supply instead); a per-industry icon needs owning every vanilla plugin (planetaryshield precedent), a per-world one is a condition icon. -> `occupation.md`
- **BUILT 2026-10-27 (jar 14:09, UNTESTED, uncommitted): THE OCCUPATION.** `ThreatOccupation` claims a held world's industries one per `occupationClaimDays` (30), `ThreatGroundWarCondition` cuts each claimed one x0 and reads its units into the front, the front grows `occupationStrataPerUnit` (1) strata a day per unit - troops while districts are contested, a pool that buds an Infestation Swarm at `occupationBudFP` (60) on `ThreatSwarmDefend` once every district is held; `colonyGroundVictory` occupies (stance entrench, "World Occupied", `$core_noDeciv` held) and never converts; a district retaken releases the newest claim; the `ThreatInfestation` card shows while anything is claimed. Knob `occupationEnabled` off = the old conquest. -> `occupation.md` 5; FIX 1 same day (the user's Arkoman save: no marker): the card shows from the first district held and an old-save front starts with its seized industries as claims, pins lifted; FIX 2: the card retired, the marker is vanilla's shortage icon from an unmet demand for the mod commodity `threatinc_infested` on each claimed industry, claimable = vanilla "industry"-tagged producers only (no stations, ports, defences); FIX 3: a district taken claims an industry at once (`catchUp`, from takeStratum and the tick), the clock claims the rest; FIX 4: the marker is PRODUCTION ("Threat Swarm" N units, Fabrication Core icon, the cut outputs drop off the list) and the claimed industry draws a dark-green image via field-less `Occupied*` subclasses of the seven vanilla producing plugins (XStream alias + setPluginClass at load, the shield precedent); FIX 5: the card stayed vanilla because a save stores an industry under its SIMPLE class name (`<Mining>`), so the alias is of the simple name now with `ensurePlugins` as the rebuild fallback each tick; the swarm icon is baked into the occupied image (2x, green box top-right - the tile's own icon row is the red shortage slot, hard-coded) and is vanilla's Fabricator sprite as an 80x80 cut-out (`graphics/threatinc/icons/threat_swarm.png`)
- **How do I replace a vanilla industry plugin with mine and keep saves portable (2026-10-27)?** `spec.setPluginClass` in onApplicationLoad for new ones, and in configureXStream `x.alias(SIMPLE_NAME, Mine.class)` - a save stores an industry element under the class's simple name (`<Mining z=...>`), not its class name, so a class-name alias matches nothing (fix 4 of the occupation, and why `migrateExistingShields` exists). Mine must declare no fields. Keep a remove/add rebuild as the fallback (`ThreatOccupation.ensurePlugins`). -> `occupation.md` fix 5
- **Why is a claimed industry "infested" but its card not green (2026-10-27)?** It drew an image variant with no green copy: vanilla swaps Farming (`farming_low/med/high`), Mining, Light Industry and Fuel Production images by condition, and `ThreatOccupation.image` only greens names in `IMAGES`. All twenty now have copies, made by `tools/occupied-images.ps1`; a new variant goes in both. -> `occupation.md` fix 6
- **BUILT 2026-10-27 evening (jar pending hw293, UNTESTED): THE HIVE POOL - an occupied world is a hive of its size without the title (`docs/occupation.md` 6).** hw292 fed the front one troop per claimed unit a day (Raesvelg 11,092 FP refilled, 934-1,552 Infestation Swarms a game, navies pinned on relief, grand fleets 3 sails); the user: "behave like a normal threat world ... shift troops from space and back to the siege at will (albeit over time) ... a bit overtuned", the claims stay. Now the claimed share of producers feeds ONE pool (`GroundFront.budPool`) at `coreStrataPerSizeMonth` x size x share x `occupationRateMult` (1), capped at `ThreatStrata.sizeAt(size)` all told; `ThreatOccupation.shift` moves it at the hive flow, one way a tick, ground first: down to the front when `fabricateNeed` > 0, up as ONE whole swarm (only if enough, at least `occupationBudFP` 60) against `hostilePointsNear` x `systemDefenceMargin` less `standingFP`, back when quiet (`absorb` after `defendLingerDays`; troops beyond `groundWant` re-embark). `occupationStrataPerUnit` gone; fields `budRising`, `orbitQuietDays`.
- **DECISION 2026-10-27 19:10 (the user, after forum confusion over proportional disruption): BACK TO VANILLA ON DISRUPTION.** Remove every custom disruption mechanic (the condition dial, `fortificationDisruptDays` / `defenseWearDays` wear clocks, the siege malus scaling, front wear, danger close, the fortification-raid rule); the swarm NEVER bombards a human world - it lands and attacks, with no effect on ground defences or military structures (they are the colony's innate defence, touched only by vanilla's own tac bomb, which then works as players expect); on a hive the strata raze that exists is THE TAC BOMB and a SAT bomb comes back: some strata plus every production building disrupted (never the Fabrication Core), much dearer than the tac bomb. Reverses the 2026-10-01 per-day engine rule for colonies. Spec: `suppression-balance.md` section 10 (v3). Kept pending the user: districts held stripping the defence figure (the ground war, not disruption), the drop toll, hulls into troops.

- **SAVE COMPATIBILITY (2026-10-27 22:58, learned the hard way):** deleting a class that saves serialise breaks every existing save (`CannotResolveClassException: threatinc.ThreatGroundFronts$HoldRefund` - the bombard-hold listener sat on every swarm fleet). Before deleting a class, grep a late save's campaign.xml for its name (`ThreatX_-Inner` for inner classes); if present keep a no-op stub (`ThreatGroundFronts.HoldRefund`). Listeners added with `addListener(x, true)` are transient and safe (`ThreatFortificationRaids`). Removing fields is fine.
- **PERFORMANCE (2026-10-27 22:50): why the late war crawls.** JFR profile of one game alone at war day 2,795: 196 -> 22 days a minute from day 315; 60% of the frame is vanilla's fleet AI weighing fleets that see each other (`TacticalModule.pickEncounterOption`, quadratic in fleets, linear in ships), 15% vanilla's per-market economy tick (281 hives), the mod's own code 7%. Levers = fewer campaign fleets: ~120 roaming 30-FP patrols, 60-90 strike fleets parked for years. Machine: 3 games x 3.6 GB leave 585 MB of 16 GB - two at a time while in use. Options 1-4 in `docs/performance.md`; the user decides.

- **Why do humans never retake an occupied world (2026-10-28 morning, the user: "that needs to be fixed")?** Three rules together, all working as built: (1) the pool refills the army to the world's counter-attack figure x fabricateHoldMargin (`ThreatGroundFronts.counterGap` via `ThreatOccupation.shift`) at the flow (400 a day) from a pool fed at a hive's rate by the world's own claimed industries and by every swarm absorbed over it, capped at a hive of the world's size (3,200 at size 4, 51,200 at size 8); (2) the NPC doctrine digs its marines in unless pushing moves the line (`doctrineDefendersPush`), and with the army always above the defence figure it never does, so landed marines sit and are ground down by the swarm's push; (3) relief fleets bombard the army at 1-2 troops a day and the pool buds a swarm against them. hw302a: 204 districts taken, 9 retaken; the pools sent 51,437 troops in 2,066 sends against 81,342 marines landed in 1,248 convoys; Cibola alone took 13,887 marines in 112 convoys against 2,325 pool troops and lost 4 districts, retook 0. An occupied world is a hive without the title (the 2026-10-27 rule) and nobody has a doctrine that retakes a hive. Options and the user's choice: `occupation.md` 7.
- **What strike fleets sit over a world for years (2026-10-28 morning)?** A landed strike's fleets stay on Defend "for as long as the front stands" (`ThreatStrikeFGI.stayOnDefend`, 2026-09-07; `ThreatSwarmDefend.tick` ends a station only when the front is gone, the market flips or leaves the economy, or the fleet is ground under defendMinStrength) - and an occupation front never ends, so until the 23:00 reabsorb they stayed for the rest of the war (the 60-90 parked fleets in the JFR profile). Since then a quiet orbit absorbs or stands them down after 10 days; what keeps an orbit busy now is the humans' own relief: a relief sails because a swarm is over the world (`reliefGoal`), bombards the army for 1-2 troops a day, the pool buds a swarm against it (`shift`, systemDefenceMargin 1.25), and the swarm over the world keeps the relief there - 3,290 "Relief over" lines and 320 buds in hw302a, stand-downs at 10-12 quiet days 50, 13-30 d 53, 31-100 d 17, 100+ d 24. Human patrols are not it (census 0 out).
- **Why do NPC defenders with the advantage not push the line back (2026-10-28, the user: "Defenders are supposed to push when they have the advantage ... That's the bug")?** They do push - hw302a logged 169 "Marines of X push" and 220 "dig in"; Cibola pushed at -0.11 and -0.16 - and dig back in within days, with the pool sending nothing in between. The doctrine (`doctrineDefendersPush`, 2026-10-24) pushes when the line rate clears `frontGrindBand` (0.1, i.e. a district every 60 days at `frontLineBaseDays` 6) computed with the defenders' cover BY DAYS (`defenderCoverIfDugIn`, 1.5 after `frontEntrenchDays` 30 dug in); pushing SPENDS those days (`tickDefenderStance` subtracts them while the stance is push), so a push that clears the band by a little loses the cover it was measured with in days, the rate falls inside the band, and the doctrine digs in - no district can complete in 60 days on 30 days of cover. Underneath, the occupation refills the army to the counter-attack figure x 1.05 (`counterGap`) at 400 a day whenever it is short, and the marines arrive 100-240 a convoy while the depot holds thousands (Salamanca 7,335 marines, 6,212 armaments, Cibola's stock 190-2,645). Cibola in the last dump: garrison ~1,355 + 191 marines, 0 armaments (dry, x0.5), against 3,246 troops at cover 1.5 holding 5 of 5. Not yet decided what to change (the user is reading).

All marines available should be brought"):** a relief for an own invaded world draws EVERYTHING every donor in reach holds above its floor, staging banks included - not the donor keep's half plus the base's siege commitment (`ThreatConvoys.spare`) that left Salamanca on 7,315 marines while Cibola took 100-240 a convoy. - **DECISION 2026-10-28 09:30 (the user, CONSUMPTION, replacing the endless occupation):** the swarm fully CONSUMES each claimed industry - progress shown on the industry like a build but meaning consumption; the industry is REMOVED when consumed and feeds no more troops; a world with nothing left to consume is abandoned (the swarm lifts off and finds another). The user's answers to the after-effects: (1) the human builder RESTORES the world to its prior state, one industry at a time, quickest to build first; (2) military structures are consumed LAST ("the most well protected"); (3) NO lump reward for a consumed industry - it pays out over time as the pool does today; (4) the number of districts held = the number of industries consumed in parallel. My defaults, not confirmed by the user: Population and the spaceport are never consumed; an industry's consumption time = its vanilla build time x `occupationConsumeMult`; the deciv hold outlives the occupation by the rebuild; lift-off = the pool and the army re-bank to the nearest hive and the strike planner picks the next target. Design: `occupation.md` 7.
- **CONSUMPTION BUILT (2026-10-28 09:28, jar 09:28:55; `occupation.md` 7):** `ThreatOccupation.consume` eats the oldest claims (as many as districts held; a military claim only once no producer stands) over the industry's vanilla build time x `occupationConsumeMult`, removes it (`market.removeIndustry`), remembers it on the market (`CONSUMED_KEY`); `share` counts consumed producers so an eaten one feeds nothing; `claimable` = `producers` then `military` (the key structures minus the station); the `Occupied*` plugins (+ `OccupiedMilitaryBase`, `OccupiedGroundDefenses`) draw vanilla's build bar as the consumption ("Consumed: N days left"); `liftOff` when nothing is left to claim - pool + troops re-embark as a swarm bound for the nearest hive (bindStrayToNearest), swarms above stand down, the world is `GUTTED_KEY` for `occupationGuttedDays` 180 (no strike targets it, deciv held), the front withdrawn; `ThreatCoreBuild.restoreStep` rebuilds consumed industries quickest-first one at a time after the yard step. Trial hw304 (round 72): the hw302 snapshots, two games, 1,500 days - industries consumed, worlds gutted / abandoned, restores, districts retaken.
